# Changelog

All notable changes to NiteOwl will be documented in this file.

## 2026-09-16 (Marketing — industry landing page differentiation, Slice 3: workflow motifs + section order + mid-page CTA, PR #125, merged and production verified)

**PR #125** (`feat/industry-presentation-slice-3`, reviewed head `762bfb993393fbd32c437f9dc8d89104716f5490`, from `main` at `d6a2cba`; **normal merge commit `3565411f81ca6760c62fedd31d2e2a2da383b1a3`** into `main`, 2026-09-16T20:57:16Z — not squash, not rebase). Deployed to production (`niteowl-29avaq7zl-niteowl-ai.vercel.app`, ● Ready, target production, carrying `niteowlhq.com` / `www.niteowlhq.com` / `niteowl-git-main`; **the build log's `Cloning … (Branch: main, Commit: 3565411)` matches the merge commit**, and every production check below was run against that exact SHA). The feature branch was deleted locally and on origin after production verification. **MARKETING PRESENTATION LAYER ONLY.** Five files, **+762 / −114**: `src/lib/site/industryPages.ts`, `src/components/marketing/IndustryPageView.tsx`, `tests/industryWorkflow.test.mjs` (new), `tests/industryPainLayouts.test.mjs`, `tests/industryPresentation.test.mjs`.

- **What it extends.** Slice 3 builds on shipped Slice 1 (heroes, PR #121) and Slice 2 (pain stories + capability priority, PR #123) **through the same typed `IndustryPage` presentation architecture and the same shared `IndustryPageView`. It does NOT create separate per-industry page implementations** — no page builder, no CMS, no client JavaScript.
- **The contract.** `IndustryPresentation` gains `workflow: "none" | "flow_curve" | "flow_circuit"` (default `none`) and `section_order: readonly IndustrySection[]` where `IndustrySection` is a **closed set of the sections that already exist** (`pain`, `workflow`, `mid_cta`, `capabilities`, `example`, `scan`, `how_it_works`, `faq`); `DEFAULT_SECTION_ORDER` is exactly the pre-Slice-3 page; the hero is always first and the final CTA always last; any shared section an order omits is appended, so no page can lose its Scan section, how-it-works or FAQ. `IndustryPage` gains typed `workflow` data (eyebrow, heading, lead, exactly four nodes, a truthfulness footnote) and `mid_cta` data (heading, body, label, href). The six existing sections moved verbatim into one keyed renderer.
- **Plumbers — `flow_curve`.** Four **round** cyan numbered nodes on an **S-curve** (inline SVG, `aria-hidden`, pointer-inert, confined to the marker row, hidden below `lg`): **Call answered → Job & address captured → Time checked against your calendar → Booking request submitted**, the last node reading "…look out for the confirmation email — which arrives only once the booking is actually made", with the footnote *A free slot is not a booking. Remy never tells a caller the booking is done, never promises that a plumber will be there at a set time, and never diagnoses the problem…* — **request-only language throughout.** Plumbing-specific section order **hero → pain timeline → workflow → mid CTA → capabilities → example → scan → how-it-works → FAQ → final**, and the mid-page Scan CTA **"See where your plumbing jobs are being lost"** immediately after the workflow.
- **Electricians — `flow_circuit`.** Four **square** amber numbered nodes on an **orthogonal, stepped circuit trace** with node squares (same SVG hygiene): **Call → Classify → Capture → Route**, where Classify is "as the caller describes it… context for you, not a diagnosis" and Route is **"where the service is one your Knowledge Base lists and a time has been checked against your calendar, Remy submits a booking request after the call; otherwise the enquiry is captured and flagged for your review — it never guesses whether you can take the job"**, with the footnote *Remy doesn't diagnose faults, certify work, decide what is safe, or send an electrician anywhere…* — **no diagnosis, certification, safety or dispatch implication.** Electrical-specific section order **hero → workflow explainer → mid CTA → pain contrast → capabilities → example → scan → how-it-works → FAQ → final**, and the mid-page Scan CTA **"See which enquiries your electrical business is losing"** immediately after the explainer, above the pain contrast.
- **Structural, not colour-only.** Curve vs stepped trace, round vs square nodes, different labels, different section sequences and different CTA positions; below `lg` both collapse to a plain vertical rail with the same numbered nodes, so the sequence reads the same without colour or SVG. Every mid-page CTA uses the same indigo button and the same bare Scan destination (`/free-tools/business-opportunity-scan`).
- **Slice 1 and Slice 2 intact.** The job-ticket / cyan and enquiry-panel / amber heroes and the timeline / contrast pain stories are pinned unchanged by the new suite; the Slice 1 / Slice 2 suites' section helpers were made order-independent.
- **Tests.** `tests/industryWorkflow.test.mjs` (22, rendered output) pins both motifs and labels, geometry and marker shape, SVG hygiene, no shared workflow presentation, the exact rendered section orders and their difference, hero-first / final-last, the default order and the omitted-section and missing-data fallbacks, the mid-CTA position and framing, every Scan CTA's href, indigo on every CTA, cross-trade wording isolation, a whole-page guard that Remy is nowhere said to have booked, dispatched, diagnosed, certified, repaired, made safe or guaranteed attendance, and no Slice 3 reference in Remy core, routes, registry, structured data or the homepage. **Focused industry / discoverability 148 / 148; full suite 2347 / 2347; `tsc --noEmit` clean; changed-file eslint clean** (repo-wide `npm run lint` unchanged at its eleven pre-existing problems in untouched dashboard / API files); **`next build` green**, both pages static.

**Production verification (2026-09-16, after the merge deployment reached Ready):** `/`, `/ai-receptionist-for-plumbers`, `/ai-receptionist-for-electricians`, `/free-tools/business-opportunity-scan` **all 200** · the live plumbers page rendering `job_ticket` + cyan, the four-stage `timeline`, `data-workflow-variant="flow_curve"` with the four approved node labels, round markers, curve path, request-only endpoint, the plumbing mid CTA and the plumbing section order, no electrical wording and no unsupported state · the live electricians page rendering `enquiry_panel` + amber, the Domestic / Commercial `contrast`, `data-workflow-variant="flow_circuit"` with Call → Classify → Capture → Route, square markers, orthogonal path with four node squares, the truthful Route wording and footnote, the electrical mid CTA and the electrical section order, no plumbing wording and no unsupported state · the two pages visibly different in geometry and structure on the live 1440 render · **the 21-viewport probe re-run against both live pages: no horizontal overflow, nothing outside the viewport, no text below 12px, every CTA single-line, workflows collapsing to vertical rails on phones** · the merge diff touching only the five files above — **routes, homepage, `publicRoutes.ts`, `structuredData.ts`, metadata, canonical URLs, JSON-LD, Remy core, voice, booking, calendar, integrations, auth / OAuth, schema, Supabase and configuration unchanged** · local `main` = `origin/main` = `3565411` by fast-forward only · **`supabase/.temp/cli-latest` was excluded from the branch, the commit and the PR, and was never staged, committed, reverted, normalised or edited at any point.** **Slice 4 (FAQ / how-it-works copy differentiation) is NOT STARTED and NOT APPROVED.** Phase B remains NOT STARTED; Stage 1 remains NOT STARTED and NOT APPROVED; the Google OAuth / Data Access review is untouched and still pending.

## 2026-09-16 (Marketing — industry landing page differentiation, Slice 2: pain layouts + capability priority / copy differentiation, PR #123, merged and production verified)

**PR #123** (`feat/industry-presentation-slice-2`, reviewed head `82b9f3ab5a41a288bb49454ef404e85e79da8137`, from `main` at `630106e`; **normal merge commit `e06d266e3d4a8911401232828bb740f02dc3b91e`** into `main`, 2026-09-16T20:16:52Z — not squash, not rebase). Deployed to production (`niteowl-oik9e9m9i-niteowl-ai.vercel.app`, ● Ready, target production, carrying `niteowlhq.com` / `www.niteowlhq.com` / `niteowl-git-main`; **the build log's `Cloning … (Branch: main, Commit: e06d266)` matches the merge commit**, and every production check below was run against that exact SHA) and verified. The feature branch was deleted locally and on origin after production verification. **MARKETING PRESENTATION LAYER ONLY.** Six files, **+625 / −94**: `src/lib/site/industryPages.ts`, `src/components/marketing/IndustryPageView.tsx`, `tests/industryPainLayouts.test.mjs` (new), `tests/industryPresentation.test.mjs`, `tests/industryElectriciansPage.test.mjs`, `tests/industryPlumbersPage.test.mjs`.

- **Why.** Slice 1 (PR #121) gave each page its own hero; the bodies still read as one template with different nouns — four of seven pain cards and three of five capability bodies were byte-identical. Slice 2 differentiates the body through the **same typed `IndustryPage` contract and the same shared `IndustryPageView`** — no per-industry view, no CMS, no client JavaScript, no SVG added.
- **The contract.** `IndustryPresentation` gains an optional `pain_layout: "cards" | "timeline" | "contrast"` (default `cards`) and `pain_points` gains optional typed `timeline` (four stages + an outcome line) and `contrast` (two columns of caller-described enquiries + a shared truth) data. A page with no `pain_layout`, or a variant without its data, renders the equal-card grid exactly as before. Light-surface theme classes (marker, rail, label, panel) are added to the static `THEME` map as literals. Capability ORDER is data-driven — the arrays were reordered, and the view is unchanged in that section.
- **Plumbers — "The job that can't wait".** Slice 1's **job ticket + cyan hero preserved.** The pain section is now a plumbing-specific **missed-call / lost-job story**: H2 *How a Plumbing Job Gets Lost While You're Under a Sink*, a **four-stage numbered timeline on a cyan rail** — *The phone rings on another job → The call goes to voicemail → They ring the next plumber → The job is gone before you call back* — with the outcome line *The missed call never shows up as a lost job — it just never shows up at all*, then **three plumbing-specific supporting cards** (*After-hours leaks don't wait for Monday · Details scribbled on a receipt in the van · Arranging the visit takes a chain of calls*). Capabilities are reordered and rewritten for the trade: *Answers while your hands are full → Gets a time checked, not just promised → Takes the job down the way you'd write it → Nothing goes missing between the call and the van → Answers the questions that interrupt every job.* **No electrician wording; no booked / confirmed / dispatched / diagnosed / certified / repaired / made-safe claim anywhere on the page.**
- **Electricians — "Every enquiry needs to be captured and understood".** Slice 1's **enquiry panel + amber hero preserved.** The pain section is now a **domestic-vs-commercial enquiry contrast**: H2 *No Two Electrical Enquiries Are the Same Call*, **two distinct amber panels** of quoted caller enquiries — *Domestic* (half the sockets in the kitchen have stopped working · the landing light flickers · the consumer unit keeps tripping · can you fit an EV charger) and *Commercial* (a quote for the electrics on a shop fit-out · a maintenance contract for two units · a rewire and board upgrade before we move in · out-of-hours commercial work) — with the shared truth that every one of them arrives while you're in a board with the power off and whoever answers has to get the details right, then **three electrician-specific supporting cards**. Capabilities are reordered and rewritten: *Captures the enquiry in the caller's words → Answers the questions you get asked every day → Answers while you're on site → Gets a time checked for the jobs you can book → Every enquiry lands in one place*, stating in the copy that Remy *doesn't diagnose the fault* and *never invents an answer or gives electrical advice*. **No plumbing wording; no diagnosis / certification / dispatch / confirmed-booking claim anywhere on the page.**
- **Differentiation now exists in two independent layers.** **Slice 1 — hero presentation:** plumbers = job ticket / cyan, electricians = enquiry panel / amber. **Slice 2 — body presentation:** plumbers = numbered missed-call timeline, electricians = domestic / commercial contrast panels. **These differences are structural and content-specific — an ordered list with numbered markers on a rail versus a two-column grid of quoted lists, different capability order, zero byte-identical lines across the two pages' pain and capability copy (pinned) — not merely colour changes.** Both remain on the same slate / indigo NiteOwl system with identical nav, typography, section rhythm and indigo CTAs.
- **Truthfulness.** Every timeline stage and every quoted enquiry describes the business's problem or the caller's words, never a Remy action; a rendered-text guard pins that Remy is nowhere said to have booked, dispatched, diagnosed, certified, repaired or made safe; a test checks that no capability was invented — every body set still maps onto answering, capture, booking request + confirmation email, Knowledge Base and lead + needs-review. Numbered markers and plain-text column titles carry the meaning without colour.
- **Tests.** `tests/industryPainLayouts.test.mjs` (22) pins both layouts, their structural difference, the cards-only fallback, the capability order per page, the absence of cross-trade wording, the truthfulness rules, the untouched Slice 1 heroes, CTAs, metadata / canonical / JSON-LD, the three homepage links per industry, exactly two industry pages, and no Remy-core or route-file reference. The existing suites fold the story copy into their claim guards. **Focused industry suite 126 / 126; full suite 2325 / 2325; `tsc --noEmit` clean; changed-file eslint clean** (repo-wide `npm run lint` unchanged at its eleven pre-existing problems in untouched dashboard / API files); **`next build` successful**, both industry pages static.

**Production verification (2026-09-16, after the merge deployment reached Ready):** `/`, `/ai-receptionist-for-plumbers`, `/ai-receptionist-for-electricians`, `/free-tools/business-opportunity-scan` **all 200** · the live plumbers page rendering `data-hero-visual="job_ticket"` with the cyan ring, `data-pain-layout-mode="timeline"` with four stages, markers 1–4, the outcome line, the cyan light cues, three supporting cards and the plumbing capability order, plumbing wording present, no electrical wording, no unsupported state anywhere on the page · the live electricians page rendering `data-hero-visual="enquiry_panel"` with the amber ring, `data-pain-layout-mode="contrast"` with the Domestic and Commercial panels, two quoted lists, the shared truth, the amber light cues, three supporting cards and the electrical capability order, electrical wording present, no plumbing wording, no unsupported state anywhere on the page · the two pages visibly differentiated in both hero and body on the live 1440 render · **the responsive probe re-run against both live pages across the existing 21 viewports** (320, 360, 375, 390, 412, 430, 480, 600, 768, 820, 1024, 1280, 1366, 1440, 1536, 1920, 2560 and landscape 844×390, 932×430, 1024×768, 1180×820): **no horizontal overflow, nothing outside the viewport, no text below 12px, every CTA single-line; the timeline becomes a vertical rail and the contrast panels stack on phones** · **Slice 2 did NOT change** the homepage, routes, `publicRoutes.ts`, `structuredData.ts`, metadata, canonical URLs, JSON-LD, Remy core, voice, booking, calendar, integrations, auth / OAuth, schema, Supabase or configuration — the merge diff touched only the six files above · local `main` = `origin/main` = `e06d266` by fast-forward only · **`supabase/.temp/cli-latest` was excluded from the branch, the commit and the PR, and was never staged, committed, reverted, normalised or edited at any point.** **Slice 3 (workflow motifs + section order + mid-page CTA) is NOT STARTED. Slice 4 (FAQ / how-it-works copy differentiation) is NOT STARTED.** Phase B remains NOT STARTED; Stage 1 remains NOT STARTED and NOT APPROVED; the Google OAuth / Data Access review is untouched and still pending.

## 2026-09-16 (Marketing — industry landing page differentiation, Slice 1: presentation contract + hero visuals, PR #121, merged and production verified)

**PR #121** (`feat/industry-presentation-slice-1`, approved head `0cc7d37a17dcfa801f1e40c0df81b098e289793c`, from `main` at `18924d4`; **normal merge commit `883893407577a94dc29ce7eea2c0c3893c35b60d`** into `main`, 2026-09-16T14:40:38Z — not squash, not rebase). Deployed to production (`niteowl-oz3orm6kk-niteowl-ai.vercel.app`, ● Ready, target production, carrying `niteowlhq.com` / `www.niteowlhq.com` / `niteowl-git-main`; **the build log's `Cloning … (Branch: main, Commit: 8838934)` matches the merge commit**) and verified. The branch was deleted locally and on origin after production verification. **MARKETING PRESENTATION LAYER ONLY.** Five files, **+639 / −41**: `src/lib/site/industryPages.ts`, `src/components/marketing/IndustryPageView.tsx`, `tests/industryPresentation.test.mjs` (new), `tests/industryElectriciansPage.test.mjs`, `tests/industryPlumbersPage.test.mjs`.

- **Why.** The two industry pages rendered the same hero visual with different nouns — 70 of the electricians page's 100 copy lines were byte-identical to the plumbers page, and the shared view had no variation point at all. Slice 1 of the approved differentiation plan makes the two heroes visibly distinct **without creating separate per-industry implementations**: still one Remy, one `IndustryPageView`, one `IndustryPage` content contract.
- **The presentation contract.** `IndustryPage` gains an optional `presentation: { theme: "indigo" | "cyan" | "amber"; hero_visual: "summary_card" | "job_ticket" | "enquiry_panel" }` and optional typed `hero.job_ticket` / `hero.enquiry_panel` data. **Closed unions only — no free-form configuration, no page builder, no client JavaScript.** The view maps `theme` to a static record of literal Tailwind classes (nothing is built at runtime, so nothing can be purged), the existing summary card is extracted unchanged as the default, and two new hero components — `JobTicket` and `EnquiryPanel` — render only when opted into. **A page with no `presentation` renders exactly as before, and a variant whose data is absent falls back to the summary card**; a test pins that everything after the hero renders byte-identically with or without presentation. A theme tints only the hero's secondary cues (eyebrow pill, the visual's ring, badges and stroke); **indigo remains the brand and CTA colour on every page**, pinned by test.
- **Plumbers — "The job that can't wait".** Cyan / teal secondary cue; the established headline *Never Miss Another Plumbing Job* kept; urgency-led, plumbing-specific bullets replace the generic four. The hero visual is now an **incoming-job ticket** — soft rounded geometry, a *Caller said: urgent* chip attributed to the caller, the problem as a quotation (*"Leaking pipe under the kitchen sink — it's dripping into the cupboard."*) captioned *in the caller's own words*, the captured rows (address, requested time in the caller's words, callback number, email), and the truthful request state **"Booking request submitted — look out for the confirmation email"**, with a curved decorative flow line.
- **Electricians — "Every enquiry, classified and captured".** Amber secondary cue; a different headline shape — *Every Electrical Enquiry, Captured While You're On Site*; capture-led bullets including *Nothing diagnosed over the phone — the details reach you, the decision stays yours*. The hero visual is now an **enquiry panel** — squared geometry, a row of **caller-described enquiry types** (Fault · Sockets & switches · Lighting · Rewire · Consumer unit · EV charger · Commercial) with one highlighted and the caption *Enquiry types as callers describe them — not a diagnosis*, boxed rows (the caller's words, *Premises — Domestic — only when the caller says so*, address, requested time, callback), and the neutral captured state **"Details captured and sent to you in the call summary"**, with an orthogonal decorative circuit trace. **The "KB-listed → booking request / otherwise → review" routing outcome was deliberately NOT encoded**, because Slice 1 cannot guarantee it as an automatic outcome; a booking request is described only where a time has actually been checked.
- **Distinguishable beyond colour.** Ticket vs panel, `rounded-3xl` vs `rounded-xl`, quotation block vs chip row, curved stroke vs orthogonal trace — on the same slate / indigo system with identical nav, typography, section rhythm and indigo CTAs. Both visuals carry an *Illustrative — not a real caller* note; decorative SVGs are inline, `aria-hidden`, `pointer-events-none`, and hidden below `sm` so they are never a layout dependency.
- **Truthfulness pinned.** Neither rendered hero contains booked, confirmed, dispatched, diagnosed, certified, scheduled or reserved as a claimed state (honest negations such as *nothing diagnosed* are allowed); no name, digit run, email address or postcode appears in either visual; each hero carries its own trade's wording and none of the other's.
- **Tests.** `tests/industryPresentation.test.mjs` (19) pins the default rendering, both opt-ins, the geometry difference, SVG hygiene, cross-trade isolation in both directions, the unsupported-state guard, no real-person data, unchanged CTAs and JSON-LD, literal theme classes, no Remy-core or route-file references and no `supabase` reference. The existing suites fold the new visual copy into the fabricated-claim and unsupported-feature guards. Full suite **2303 / 2303**; `tsc --noEmit` clean; changed-file eslint clean; `next build` renders both pages static.

**Production verification (2026-09-16, after the merge deployment reached Ready):** `/`, `/ai-receptionist-for-plumbers`, `/ai-receptionist-for-electricians`, `/free-tools/business-opportunity-scan` **all 200** · the live plumbers hero rendering `data-hero-visual="job_ticket"` with the cyan pill and ring, the plumbing headline and bullets, the quotation, the urgency chip, the booking-request state and the curved stroke, no electrician wording and no unsupported state · the live electricians hero rendering `data-hero-visual="enquiry_panel"` with the amber pill and ring, the new headline, all seven caller-described chips with one highlighted, the *not a diagnosis* caption, the premises row, the captured state and the circuit trace, no plumbing wording and no unsupported state · all four CTAs per page unchanged (Scan ×3, `/`), titles, canonicals and WebPage + FAQPage JSON-LD unchanged, primary CTAs still indigo, the homepage still carrying three links per industry · the 21-viewport probe re-run against both live pages — no horizontal overflow, no element outside the viewport, no text under 12px, every CTA single-line, the visuals stacking cleanly on phones with the SVGs hidden · the merge diff touching only the five files above — routes, homepage, `publicRoutes.ts`, `structuredData.ts`, Remy core, booking, voice, calendar, integrations, auth / OAuth, Supabase and schema unchanged · local `main` = `origin/main` = `8838934` by fast-forward only · **`supabase/.temp/cli-latest` was excluded from the branch, the commit and the PR, and was never staged, committed, reverted, normalised or edited at any point.** **Slice 2 (pain layouts) is NOT STARTED**; Slices 3 and 4 are not started. Phase B remains NOT STARTED; Stage 1 remains NOT STARTED and NOT APPROVED; the Google OAuth / Data Access review is untouched and still pending.

## 2026-09-16 (Marketing — industry FAQ heading rendered from content, PR #119, merged and production verified)

**PR #119** (`fix/industry-faq-heading`, approved head `1126ac8b0bde5f0f239c3773ca326b51efd9f062`, from `main` at `9a17b2c`; **normal merge commit `b731acd24687c5cdaebe1c726516b61dacae009c`** into `main`, 2026-09-16T12:42:17Z — not squash, not rebase). Deployed to production (`niteowl-c27o459jx-niteowl-ai.vercel.app`, ● Ready, target production, carrying `niteowlhq.com` / `www.niteowlhq.com` / `niteowl-git-main`; **the build log's `Cloning … (Branch: main, Commit: b731acd)` matches the merge commit**) and verified. The branch was deleted locally and on origin after production verification. **CONTENT / PRESENTATION ONLY.** Four files, **+47 / −1**: `src/lib/site/industryPages.ts`, `src/components/marketing/IndustryPageView.tsx`, `tests/industryElectriciansPage.test.mjs`, `tests/industryPlumbersPage.test.mjs`.

- **The defect.** PR #118's production verification found that `/ai-receptionist-for-electricians` rendered the FAQ heading **"Questions plumbers ask about Remy"**. The string was hard-coded in the shared `IndustryPageView`, not in the industry content object, so the content-level tests — which read the constant, not the render — could not see it. Everything else on the page was electrician-specific.
- **The fix.** `IndustryPage` gains a required `faq_heading: string`; `PLUMBERS_PAGE` keeps *Questions plumbers ask about Remy*, `ELECTRICIANS_PAGE` gets *Questions electricians ask about Remy*, and the view renders `{page.faq_heading}` in place of the literal — one line. No special-casing, no duplicated view, no other content changed; TypeScript makes the field mandatory for any future industry. **The rule this establishes: industry-specific wording belongs in the industry content object, never in the shared view.**
- **Tests now read the rendered HTML, not the constant.** Each page carries its own heading and not the other's; the electricians render contains no plumbing wording and the plumbers render no electrical wording anywhere; the view source reads `page.faq_heading` and contains no `Questions … ask about Remy` literal and no industry word. **Mutation check:** with only the view and content reverted, four of the new tests fail; with the fix, the full suite is **2284 / 2284**. `tsc --noEmit` and changed-file eslint clean; `next build` renders both industry pages static.

**Production verification (2026-09-16, after the merge deployment reached Ready):** `/`, `/ai-receptionist-for-plumbers`, `/ai-receptionist-for-electricians`, `/free-tools/business-opportunity-scan` **all 200** · the plumbers page renders *Questions plumbers ask about Remy* and no electrical wording; the electricians page renders *Questions electricians ask about Remy* and no plumbing wording · both pages' titles and all four CTAs unchanged · the homepage *Built for your industry* section still exactly Plumbers → Electricians with three links per industry · the merge diff touched only the four files above — homepage, `publicRoutes.ts`, sitemap, Remy core, voice, booking, calendar, integrations, auth / OAuth, schema and Supabase unchanged · local `main` = `origin/main` = `b731acd` by fast-forward only · **`supabase/.temp/cli-latest` was excluded from the branch, the commit and the PR, and was never staged, committed, reverted, normalised or edited at any point.** Phase B remains NOT STARTED; Stage 1 remains NOT STARTED and NOT APPROVED; the Google OAuth / Data Access review is untouched and still pending.

## 2026-09-16 (Marketing — second industry landing page, electricians, PR #118, merged and production verified)

**PR #118** (`feat/electricians-industry-landing-page`, approved head `e5bfb0708027fc6ca0da332b94d178e2292fee6c`, from `main` at `569e2a0`; **normal merge commit `9a17b2ca288031ee6e3a5cec67cce400eeab2a34`** into `main`, 2026-09-16T12:11:21Z — not squash, not rebase). Deployed to production (`niteowl-5be6lq3ga-niteowl-ai.vercel.app`, ● Ready, target production, carrying `niteowlhq.com` / `www.niteowlhq.com` / `niteowl-git-main`; **the build log's `Cloning … (Branch: main, Commit: 9a17b2c)` matches the merge commit**) and verified. The feature branch was deleted locally and on origin after production verification. **MARKETING / ACQUISITION ONLY**, explicitly approved during the Google OAuth wait state; it is not Remy V1, V1.1, Phase B or Stage 1 work. Seven files: two added, five modified, **+558 / −13**.

- **Route and purpose.** **`/ai-receptionist-for-electricians`** — the second industry-specific Remy **marketing** landing page, built exactly on the plumbers pattern: one more content constant (`ELECTRICIANS_PAGE` in `src/lib/site/industryPages.ts`) rendered by the **unchanged** shared `IndustryPageView`, mounted by a 39-line `src/app/ai-receptionist-for-electricians/page.tsx` cloned from the plumbers route. **There is still one Remy.** Nothing is keyed by industry; no voice, booking, calendar, lead-capture, auth, schema, OAuth or Scan-logic file changed, and tests pin that `assistant.ts`, `calls.ts`, `vapi.ts` and the view contain no industry keying. **The reusable pattern is now proven across two industries.**
- **Electrician-specific, truthful copy.** Hero *Never Miss Another Electrical Job*; pain points about fault calls arriving mid-job, after-hours enquiries and the questions that interrupt work ("Do you fit EV chargers? Do you do rewires?"); service categories named only as general classes — faults, sockets and switches, lighting, rewires, fuse boards / consumer units, EV chargers, installations — and always *in the caller's own words*; an illustrative call ("Half the sockets in my kitchen have stopped working and the board keeps tripping…") in which Remy explicitly does not try to diagnose the fault; seven FAQs. **Remy is never said to diagnose, test, certify or make safe electrical work** — a regex guard pins it. Booking is a *request* until the calendar accepts it, services must be in the Knowledge Base, calendar sync needs a connected calendar, urgency is flagged and never a promised response time, Remy does not transfer live calls, life-threatening emergencies go to 999. No customer, testimonial, statistic, figure, benchmark, image or placeholder.
- **Acquisition path.** Industry page → the unchanged nine-question Business Opportunity Scan → Remy: hero primary, Scan-section and closing CTAs are the literal `/free-tools/business-opportunity-scan`; the secondary CTA reuses the plumbers page's destination (`/`, where the walkthrough player lives) — no new walkthrough mechanism; problem links are the three A-2a pages. Every `href` is pinned to a real `page.tsx`.
- **Homepage.** The *Built for your industry* section now lists **exactly Plumbers → Electricians** (CTA *Explore Remy for Electricians →*); the existing *Perfect For* ⚡ Electricians card links to the page; the footer gains *AI Receptionist for Electricians* beside the plumbers link. Six files: four added, two modified, **+1096 / −2**.

- **Route and purpose.** **`/ai-receptionist-for-plumbers`** — the first industry-specific Remy **marketing** landing page. A vertical framing (hero, plumbing pain points, five capability cards, an illustrative call example, the Scan section, a four-step *how it works*, seven plumber-specific FAQs, a closing CTA) in the homepage's existing visual language. **There is still one Remy.** Nothing is keyed by industry: no voice, booking, calendar, lead-capture, prompt, schema, auth, settings or OAuth file changed, and a test pins that `assistant.ts`, `calls.ts` and `vapi.ts` contain no industry keying.

The hero primary, Scan-section and closing CTAs are all the literal `/free-tools/business-opportunity-scan` — no query string, hash, prefill or carried state. Problem links are the three A-2a pages. Every `href` is pinned to a real `page.tsx` by test.
- **Truthfulness.** Every claim is a shipped behaviour and is conditional where the behaviour is: a voice booking is a **request** until the calendar accepts it (the PR #30 closing rule); services must be in the Knowledge Base; calendar sync needs a connected calendar; urgency is flagged and never a promised response time; Remy does not transfer live calls; life-threatening emergencies are directed to 999. *How it works* step 4 is *review in your dashboard*, not outcome measurement, which is not built. **No customer, company, testimonial, logo, statistic, figure, benchmark or image is invented** — social proof is omitted rather than placeholdered — and the call example is labelled illustrative and names nobody. ## 2026-09-15 (Architecture — Part XV Outcome Learning and Cross-Product Decision Intelligence closeout, PR #107, merged and production verified)

**PR #107** (`docs/architecture-part-xv-outcome-learning`, approved head `c9fe08340f9b14bed6ef13c0f690d86323c23e2d`, from `main` at `15ca468`; **normal merge commit `3f292c1f7161dea5f9a01e8b0d5f77c9584c8ce9`** into `main`, 2026-09-15T14:02:53Z — not squash, not rebase, two parents `15ca468` + `c9fe083`). Deployed to production (`dpl_BQUrehi3S8MSqwvgAndfQJiiqyeA`, ● Ready, target production, carrying `niteowlhq.com` / `www.niteowlhq.com`; **the build log's `Cloning … (Branch: main, Commit: 3f292c1)` matches the merge commit**) and verified. The branch was deleted locally and on origin after production verification. **DOCUMENTATION ONLY: `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` is the sole file in the PR** — one appended hunk, **+476 / −0**, Part XV at §§109–115. No existing architecture text was altered and §21 is not redrawn.

- **The reconciliation is the main finding.** The fifteenth review asked for a canonical Outcome Learning and Cross-Product Decision Intelligence layer; the honest answer is that it is **overwhelmingly already canonical** — Part III built the records, Parts IV and V made them honest, **Part VII was written against a near-identical brief and answered sixteen of its twenty-four sections with "already exists"**, Part IX made the ownership invariants binding, Part XIII closed the provenance half. §109.1 places every requirement against what exists; §109.2 records **twelve explicit refusals**.
- **"NiteOwl Decision Intelligence" is a name, not a component.** It denotes the coordinated operation of **existing layers 4, 5, 6 and 9** — the Outcome Spine, Decision & Outcome Memory, Provenance and the Learning Layer — under the existing invariants (derived never primary; dependencies downward only; rent the computation, own the corpus, recipe and evaluation; one canonical `DecisionRecord`; an inference never silently becomes a fact; confidence is not authority). **No new service, component, runtime, layer, store, database, table or second system of record.** The canonical **`DecisionRecord` (§20.7), Decision Provenance (§93) and Outcome Provenance (§94, §95) are unchanged**, as is §24's Cross-Product Learning Contract (one clause added to exchange type 3 at §110.5; nothing removed).
- **M20 — Learned Pattern contract (§110).** A learned generalisation had no carrier: §23's tiers are per-**link**, §57.2's levels are per-**scope**, §42.2's Finding is per-**tenant-window**, and §58.1 governs a derived artefact's rights but not what it asserts. Defined as a **fifth profile of the existing §58.1 derived artefact** — no table, no store, no record type — inheriting recipe, corpus boundary, `evidence_scope`, consents and rebuild-without, and adding `assertion`, `maturity` (`observation` → `repeated_correlation` → `weak_pattern` → `supported_pattern` → `validated_rule`), `support`, `contradicting_evidence[]`, `valid_until` and `supersedes` / `superseded_by`. Five rules: **no automatic promotion**, and `validated_rule` only on one of §23's five admissible causal bases; no denominator means `observation`; **decay by re-measurement, never an arithmetic half-life** — an expired pattern is stale, not false; contradiction lowers and never deletes; and **self-citation is prohibited**. It proposes and never writes facts (§20.9 unchanged).
- **M21 — support independence (§111).** `support_independence` — `independent` / `mixed` / `niteowl_influenced` — **derived from the provenance of the supporting population, never manually asserted and never a flag**, with a **`validated_rule` ceiling** where the support is wholly NiteOwl-influenced, and the limitation shown on every surface that displays such a pattern. It closes the one bias no existing safeguard reached: NiteOwl recommends, owners adopt, the corpus fills with cases NiteOwl put there, and support grows with every repetition of its own advice — with nothing edited and no defect to find. §55.1's denominator, §56.1's withheld population, §54.2's evaluation boundary and §23's tiers are **unchanged and unweakened**.
- **M22 — retention by record class (§112).** **Retention is declared with the record class before the first row of that class is written, never as a per-row judgement afterwards** — §20.5's payload-whitelist construction applied to time — across raw observations / Spine events, decisions (with the whitelisted input digest as the shorter-lived identifying surface), actions, outcomes, measured impact, learned patterns, and **superseded learning, retained for explanation and excluded from learning**. **No regulatory period is invented.** Erasure-by-reference (M7), provenance continuity, non-identifying history and every existing privacy and consent boundary are preserved.
- **The continuous end-to-end trace (§113).** One coherent 4–6 PM missed-call trace — observe → business state → diagnose → explain → size → recommend → route → withhold → authorise → act → measure outcome → measure impact → learn → cross-product reuse — using only carriers that already exist. It previously existed only in fragments across §22, §59.2 and §98.1.
- **Implementation sequence recorded, NOT started (§114).** Stage 0 is this documentation. Stage 1 — the smallest real write, one append-only tenant-scoped `business_events` mechanism beginning with `appointment.booked` at the existing booking choke point, references and non-identifying payload, `dedupe_key`, both `occurred_at` and `recorded_at`, **no read path back into Remy** — is **not begun and not approved**; later stages (DecisionRecord capture, outcome resolution, aggregate evaluation, the Learned Pattern profile, cross-product / cohort / experimentation) likewise. Triggers stay qualitative and unchanged — first paying business and sufficient live use, a reliable production booking path, enough real resolved outcomes — and **no numeric threshold is invented**. **NOW: none** — three PREPARE items (P52–P54), one LATER (L36).
- **No implementation of any kind, and Phase B remains NOT STARTED.** No code, schema, migration, table, store, service, layer, runtime, route, UI, flag, prompt, test, provider or configuration change. **Remy V1 untouched** — no new Remy event, storage, flag, provider integration, booking or voice behaviour, and **attendance / no-show learning and caller sentiment explicitly not introduced and not recommended** (§115.3). The Scan, auth, Supabase, calendar, onboarding, middleware and providers are unchanged.
- **The risk that now dominates**, recorded at §115.1: **the architecture is sufficiently mature that premature implementation is a larger risk than missing architecture.** This was the fifteenth such review, and the next milestone is still Google's verification review.

**Verification:** `git diff --check` clean; the diff purely additive (one hunk, +476 / −0, zero deleted lines); the appended block normalised to CRLF so `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` remained uniformly CRLF (9505 CRLF, 0 bare LF); an audit confirmed every occurrence of *"Decision Intelligence service"*, *"cross-tenant"*, *"automatic promotion"* and *"chain-of-thought"* in the new text sits inside a **prohibition or a mitigation**, with no "Decision Intelligence database / table", no second `DecisionRecord` and no automatic maturity promotion anywhere. No test or build suite was run, because no code changed.

**Production verification (2026-09-15, after the merge deployment reached Ready):** PR #107 merged by **normal merge commit** with two parents · deployment **Ready**, target production, **build commit SHA-verified equal to the merge commit** from the build log · `/api/health` **200** `{"status":"ok","database":"ok"}` · `/`, `/free-tools`, `/free-tools/business-opportunity-scan`, `/free-tools/lost-revenue`, `/free-tools/problems/unanswered-enquiries`, `/free-tools/problems/enquiries-that-do-not-book`, `/free-tools/problems/booking-back-and-forth`, `/sitemap.xml`, `/robots.txt` — **all 200** (ten checks, all green) · local `main` = `origin/main` = `3f292c1` by fast-forward only · Part XV confirmed present on `main` at `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` line 9033 with §§110–115 · feature branch deleted locally and remotely · **`supabase/.temp/cli-latest` was excluded from the branch, the commit and the PR, and was never staged, committed, reverted, normalised or edited at any point.**

- **Implementation.** `src/lib/site/lostRevenuePage.ts` (new, pure): static content only, importing only the `enquiry.unanswered` headline from `scanRecommendations` and `SCAN_PATH` / the problem-page path from the A-2a content module. `src/app/free-tools/lost-revenue/page.tsx` (new): server component with the A-1 canonical / OpenGraph / Twitter metadata pattern and `WebPage` JSON-LD from the existing shared builder (brand-only publisher). `src/lib/site/publicRoutes.ts` registers the route — **sitemap and robots consume the registry unchanged; no change to `sitemap.ts` or `robots.ts` logic** — so the **exact public-route / sitemap count rises from 9 to 10**. The `/free-tools` hub gains **one internal Lost Revenue link** beneath *Common problems* (*"Want to size missed work? Start from the Lost Revenue page →"*) — not a fourth card; the Scan and Setup Kit cards and the three problem links are unchanged.
- **Wording provenance.** The sized problem is named by `SCAN_RECOMMENDATIONS["enquiry.unanswered"].headline` verbatim. Three prose sections — *How the estimate is built*, *When it will say it can't*, *An estimate, not a measurement* — restate the canonical sizing ideas **without importing or executing the engine**: missed enquiries assumed to have converted at about the same rate as answered ones (and that this is likely generous), the visitor's own typical job value, a range worked out from those answers alone, the UNKNOWN gates in their own terms (*"not sure"* on any operand, *"no way of knowing when an enquiry was missed"*, *"the counts do not fit together"*, a range *"so wide that it would tell you nothing"*), and that the result is an estimate from what the visitor told it — not a measurement, not a comparison with anyone else, not a promise of what would come back. Each idea is pinned by test so the prose cannot drift into a different economic claim. **No number appears on the page.** `why_it_matters` is not echoed.
- **Truthfulness and anti-funnel guarantees, all pinned by `tests/organicLostRevenueEntry.test.mjs` (23).** Route registered and in the sitemap, not private; robots deep-equal to A-1; not a fourth problem page (exactly three directories remain under `problems/`). Server-only: no `"use client"`, hook, request API, `cookies()`/`headers()`/`searchParams`, `document`/`window`/storage, `fetch`, Supabase, `after()`, analytics name, external host, `<script src>`, form, email, clock or id generation; no new dependency. **No duplicate sizing or calculation logic and no engine call**: no `deriveFindings` / `computeLostRevenue` / `recommendFor` / `deriveHypotheses` / `buildScanReport` / `validateScanAnswers`, no reference to `scanLostRevenue` / `scanFindings` / `scanHypotheses` / `E1_` / `EstimateBasis`, and a string-stripped detector refusing arithmetic, thresholds and answer comparisons. **UNKNOWN stated as "a real answer, not a failure"**; **estimate-not-measurement language required present**; ban-lists over rendered text, metadata and JSON-LD refuse statistics, `%`, currency-plus-number, benchmarks, industry averages, *"most businesses"*, *"businesses like yours"*, customer counts, reviews, ratings, testimonials, locations, *"calculator"*, measurement claims, diagnosis (*"you are losing"*, *"your business is"*, *"because your"*, *"root cause"*, *"you will / would recover"*, *"definitely"*) and pressure (sign up, log in, pricing, trial, urgency, buy now, upgrade); **no product named, no Remy**. One wording change during test-driving is recorded: *"not a promise of what you would recover"* became *"not a promise of what would come back"* so a context-free diagnosis ban cannot fire on a negated clause; the meaning is unchanged and the new phrase is pinned.
- **No questionnaire, version or engine change.** `SCAN_QUESTIONS.length === 9`, the six required ids (`q1_channels`, `q2_reachable`, `q3_enquiries_per_week`, `q5_miss_visibility`, `q6_followup`, `q7_messages_to_book`), `SCAN_QUESTION_SET_VERSION`, `SCAN_RULE_SET_VERSION`, `SCAN_PRIORITISATION_RULE_SET_VERSION`, `SCAN_CLUSTER_RULE_SET_VERSION` and `SCAN_HYPOTHESIS_RULE_SET_VERSION` all still `v1` (pinned); `validateScanAnswers` and `buildScanReport` called only from `ScanClient.tsx`, which still reads no entry / mode / prefill parameter. **Zero diff** in `src/lib/freetools/*`, `ScanClient.tsx`, `scanPresentation.ts`, the Scan page, the Setup Kit, `problemPages.ts`, `ProblemPageView.tsx`, the three A-2a pages, `sitemap.ts`, `robots.ts`, `structuredData.ts`, middleware, auth, Supabase, Remy, calendar and onboarding. `tests/organicDiscoverability.test.mjs`'s exact sitemap list extended from nine to ten URLs — its title and two comment lines are the three deleted lines in the PR.
- **The standing A-2 decision is preserved:** the existing nine-question Scan is not shortened or replaced; no separate Lost Revenue questionnaire exists or may be introduced without an explicit `SCAN_QUESTION_SET_VERSION` decision; the Lost Revenue page is framing / entry only; the Business Opportunity Scan remains the assessment and report engine.

**PR #101** (`feat/organic-discoverability-a1`, approved head `840657ec15ec7d673a4c20ca3467e0b250523eee`, from `main` at `3da634c`; **normal merge commit `fcd2e68234479a4d5f0e88dbb1a0df3583cfa7d8`** into `main`, 2026-09-14T17:40:02Z). Deployed to production (● Ready, target production, branch `main`, carrying `niteowlhq.com` / `www.niteowlhq.com`; **the build log's `Cloning … (Branch: main, Commit: fcd2e68)` matches the merge commit**) and verified. The feature branch was deleted locally and on origin after production verification, with `git branch -d` accepting it as merged. **This is the first increment of the Organic Acquisition Engine — an extension of the existing free-product architecture, not a second one** (`PROJECT_CONTEXT.md` *Free-Product Strategy* §7 records the engine, its binding rules and the A–D phase ladder). A read-only reconciliation of the proposal against `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` §26, §72.2, §76–§78, §84.3 and `docs/AGENT_ACCESS_LAYER.md` §25 preceded it and found no conflict. Nine files: five added, four modified, **+759 / −15**. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` is unchanged.

- **Canonical public-route inventory** — `src/lib/site/publicRoutes.ts` (new, pure): six indexable pages (`/`, `/free-tools`, `/free-tools/business-opportunity-scan`, `/free-tools/ai-receptionist-setup-kit`, `/privacy`, `/terms`) and fifteen private route families (`/dashboard /chat /leads /calendar /knowledge /settings /onboarding /admin /api /auth /login /signup /reset-password /forgot-password /booking` — the manage-booking link needs no login but is token-gated, so it is private). One list read by the sitemap, robots and the tests. No clock, request, cookie, storage or store.
- **`/sitemap.xml`** — `src/app/sitemap.ts`, the Next file convention (the installed docs under `node_modules/next/dist/docs/…/01-metadata/` were read first). A pure map over the inventory. **No `lastModified`**: no reliable per-route modification date exists — a route is a layout plus components plus shared modules — and a date that is not known is not stated: not `new Date()`, not a file mtime, not a hand-typed stamp. The output is byte-identical for every call and every visitor.
- **`/robots.txt`** — `src/app/robots.ts`: `User-Agent: *`, `Allow: /`, the fifteen families disallowed, `Sitemap: https://niteowlhq.com/sitemap.xml`. The disallow list *is* the inventory's private list, so the two files cannot disagree.
- **Per-free-tool metadata** — `/free-tools` and both tool pages gain their own `alternates.canonical`, OpenGraph (`type`, absolute `url`, `siteName`, title, description) and Twitter card; titles and descriptions are unchanged. Previously the tool pages inherited Remy's OpenGraph title and description from the root layout.
- **Truthful JSON-LD** — `src/lib/site/structuredData.ts` (new): `WebApplication` on each tool (`applicationCategory: BusinessApplication`, `isAccessibleForFree: true`, `offers { price: "0" }`) and `CollectionPage` on the hub, its `hasPart` **derived from the hub's own `TOOLS` list** so it can only name a tool the page actually links to. **Publisher is the brand — `{"@type":"Organization","name":"NiteOwl AI","url":"https://niteowlhq.com"}` — not a legal entity.** The repository's legal pages consistently name `NiteOwl AI Ltd`, so that name was supported, but structured data needs a publisher, not a registration, and the smallest truthful form introduces no legal-name claim anywhere new; a test refuses `Ltd` / `Limited` / `Holdings` / company-number text in the tool JSON-LD. The shape has no field for a rating, review, count, saving, benchmark or location; the only numeral is the zero price. Rendered as a script string child with `<` written as `<` (React 19 renders script text verbatim) — **no `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`**, so the Scan surface's injection guard stays intact.
- **Internal linking verified, not changed** — hub → both tools and home; the free-tools layout nav → hub and home on every tool page; footer → privacy and terms. No funnel, handoff, routing service or middleware added.
- **Zero persistence, zero identity, zero tracking/analytics, zero provider dependency.** No cookie, `localStorage`/`sessionStorage`, `cookies()`/`headers()`, `searchParams`, `fetch`, Supabase, `after()`, analytics name, external host, `<script src>` or new package in any A-1 file — each pinned by test. **No change to Scan intelligence** (`src/lib/freetools` zero diff), **no change to Remy**, **no change to routing or recommendations**, no contact capture, no share link, no problem page, **no Lost-Revenue entry page yet**, no A-2.
- **One reviewed, approved test extension.** `tests/freeToolsScanSurface.test.mjs`'s import allow-list for the Scan surface gains a single alternative, `@/lib/site/(publicRoutes|structuredData)`, so the Scan page can use the shared JSON-LD builder instead of duplicating its shape. Extended, not loosened: every other specifier is still refused and the `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` guard is untouched.
- **`tests/organicDiscoverability.test.mjs` (34 tests).** The sitemap exposes exactly the six public URLs, absolute, with no query or fragment; every public path is a real `page.tsx`; **the public and private lists partition the real `src/app` inventory**, so an unclassified new page fails before a crawler can find it; every route handler is private; robots disallows every family and no public route; `isPrivatePath` is segment-prefix, not substring; sitemap and robots deep-equal across calls; the no-identity / no-storage / no-provider / no-external-host sweep over every A-1 file; site modules import only `next` types and each other; no new dependency; JSON-LD cannot express `AggregateRating` / `Review` / `ratingValue` / `FAQPage` / location / interaction keys (output and builder source); the hub renders exactly one parseable JSON-LD block listing both tools; metadata canonical / OpenGraph / Twitter agree per page and carry no `%`, currency-plus-number, "businesses like yours", "on average", customer-count, superlative or Remy pitch; the hub has no form, email field, sign-up gate or pricing push; no Scan engine is called anywhere under `src/app` or `src/lib/site` except the two tool surfaces; the existing anti-funnel suite is unchanged.
