## 2026-09-14 (Business Opportunity Scan — deterministic diagnostic hypotheses, PR F delivered as PR #99, merged and production verified)

**PR #99** (`feat/scan-hypotheses`, approved head `2a3baf8a67b74e3658cdb1e20b04b1953db8d567`, from `main` at `56a1be7`; **normal merge commit `33171f015bdddec0c28a67058dfc16d23c226ff1`** into `main`, 2026-09-14T14:28:26Z). Deployed to production (● Ready, target production, carrying `niteowlhq.com` / `www.niteowlhq.com`; **SHA-verified** — the build log opens `Cloning … (Branch: main, Commit: 33171f0)`) and verified: `/api/health` HTTP 200 `{"status":"ok","database":"ok"}`, homepage 200, `/free-tools` 200, `/free-tools/business-opportunity-scan` 200, and the string *"What might be behind this"* located in the live client bundle (`/_next/static/chunks/12i2punxwwwp-.js`), so the layer is genuinely deployed rather than merely routable. The feature branch was deleted locally and on origin **after** production verification succeeded. **This is PR F, the Part XIV §103 Hypothesis contract — the last of the six Part XIV contracts and the only remaining gap in the loop's Explain stage.** Nine files: two added, seven modified, **+1060 / −5**. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` is unchanged — §103 was the contract, and this is the build against it.

- **A hypothesis is a candidate explanation, and that is its ceiling (§103).** New pure module `src/lib/freetools/scanHypotheses.ts` (imports `scanTypes` only) and `tests/freeToolsScanHypotheses.test.mjs` (41 tests). Seven extended additively: `scanTypes.ts` (`ScanHypothesis`, `ScanHypothesisCode`, `ScanHypothesisRankReason`, `ScanHypothesesEmptyReason`, `SCAN_HYPOTHESIS_RULE_SET_VERSION`, two `ScanReportFinding` fields, one `ScanReport` field), `scanFindings.ts` (**+7 / −0** — one import, one constant, `...deriveHypotheses(finding.condition, answers)` in the per-finding map, the version on the report), `scanPresentation.ts`, `ScanClient.tsx`, `tests/freeToolsScanBoundary.test.mjs` (module list 12 → 13), `tests/freeToolsScanSurface.test.mjs` (exact footer line extended) and `tests/freeToolsScanAntiFunnel.test.mjs`.
- **The contract as shipped.** `hypothesis_id` (`${condition}:${code}`, deterministic, never positional) · **`claim_class: "hypothesis"` as a literal** — `asserted_cause` and `observation` are unrepresentable, so P50's flattening cannot happen to a value that never had another class · `code` from a closed union of six · `evidence: ScanEvidenceRef[]` with **at least one owner answer quoted verbatim** as `business_provided` · `contradicting_evidence` (empty — no Phase 1 rule defines one, and the field exists so the absence is a stated empty) · `source_type: "derived_deterministic"` · `hypothesis_confidence`, **its own, inherited from nothing** · `rank` 1..n with an enumerated `rank_reason` (`more_evidence` / `rule_table_order`; the first is declared and pinned never to bind in Phase 1, since every rule cites exactly one answer) · fixed `display_text`. Per finding: `hypotheses` (never null) and `hypotheses_empty_reason: "no_rule_matched" | null`.
- **The closed rule table.** `enquiry.unanswered`: `reachability_window_limited` (Q2 working hours, medium), `reachability_inconsistent` (Q2 varies, low), `channels_spread` (Q1 ≥ 3 channels, low), `misses_go_unnoticed` (Q5 no / sometimes, medium). `booking.friction`: `async_channel_back_and_forth` (Q1 includes text/WhatsApp, email, web form or social, medium), `replies_wait_for_availability` (Q2 working hours / varies, low). **`enquiry.no_followup` has no rules, deliberately** — Q6 *is* the practice and nothing else the owner said explains it — so it always produces `[]` with `no_rule_matched`, and nothing is invented to fill it. **Q1 and Q2 are read only as evidence for an explanation**; Q1/Q2-aware routing (§87.2) remains deferred and no recommendation changed.
- **Confidence independence is structural.** `deriveHypotheses(condition, answers)` takes the condition, not the finding, so `finding_confidence` and `confidence_cap_reason` are unreadable from where a hypothesis is decided. Pinned in both directions: Q5 = *no* caps the finding at low while the working-hours rule emits medium; Q5 = *yes, always* with Q2 = *varies* gives a high finding and a low hypothesis.
- **Tentative wording only — an explanatory layer, not proof of causation.** Every display text and the rendered section use *may / could / suggests*, and a test refuses *because / caused / cause of / root cause / is why*. The empty case renders *"Your answers don't point to a particular reason."* The Atlas boundary (§100.3) is untouched: the Scan still asserts no cause and reasons across no domain.
- **Presentation.** One *"What might be behind this"* block inside each existing finding card, between *How confident we are* and *What it may be worth*: the note, then one item per hypothesis with its rank, the answer it rests on, its own confidence and the rule that placed it. No new top-level section, CTA, link, product, handoff or storage; the footer gains *explanation rules v1*. The surface holds no rule of its own.
- **Strictly downstream, and structurally so.** Only the assembler imports the module. `deriveFindings`, `computeLostRevenue`, `prioritise`, `deriveDependencies`, `deriveEvidenceGaps`, `recommendFor` and `deriveClusters` neither import it nor take hypotheses. **`shared_cause_candidate` is NOT activated**: `scanClusters.ts` does not import hypotheses, and a test pins that the relation never fires even where two findings' hypotheses overlap on the same answer. It is the natural follow-on and its own decision.
- **Upstream non-interference proven, not assumed.** Across fourteen scenarios (including a suppressed Q4 > Q3 finding and a Q3 *not sure*) every pre-existing report field — findings, impact, recommendation, impact class, funnel, earliest leak, dependencies, prioritisation, evidence gaps, clusters, inconsistencies — was recomputed from the shipped modules and deep-compared against the report; a key-set pin shows the only new keys are the two per-finding fields and `hypothesis_rule_set_version`; finding order stays `SCAN_CONDITION_ORDER` and the count is unchanged; `SCAN_RULE_SET_VERSION`, `SCAN_PRIORITISATION_RULE_SET_VERSION` and `SCAN_CLUSTER_RULE_SET_VERSION` did not move. All five deleted lines audited: in-place widenings only (footer clause, import allow-list, destructuring); no pre-existing assertion weakened.
- **Boundary.** No clock, randomness, identifier generation, network, storage, tenant, provider or model reachable; the boundary suite now covers thirteen modules.

**Verification:** hypotheses suite **41 pass / 0 fail**; all Scan suites **492 / 0**; full repository **2139 pass / 0 fail / 0 skipped** (367 suites); `tsc --noEmit` clean; ESLint **0 problems in the nine changed files**, repository baseline unchanged at **11 (7 errors, 4 warnings)**; `next build` successful with the Scan route still static / prerendered; `git diff --check` clean; every changed file consistently CRLF. **Six mutation checks, each verified to have landed and then completely restored (post-restore 170 / 170 green):** an emitted hypothesis with no evidence ref → 10 fail; `claim_class` changed from `"hypothesis"` → 2; *because* introduced into a display text → 2; confidence copied from the finding → 4; findings reordered → 3; a hypothesis invented for the empty state → 4.

**What PR #99 did NOT do, and what its merge does not approve:** `shared_cause_candidate` activation · scenario output (**L35**) · `evidence_refs[].role` (**P49**) · persistence, run identity, bearer-token linkage and the consent and promotion flow (Part XII §89) · outcome or impact measurement · any Part XIII provenance runtime · cross-product learning · benchmarks or cohorts · Q1/Q2-aware routing · Setup Kit pre-fill · the standalone Lost Revenue entry · a second business process · a fourth condition code · any model call or prompt · any Remy, auth, Supabase, database or calendar change · and every later Scan phase. **PR F is complete and closed.** All six Part XIV contracts are now built; whatever the next Scan increment is, it needs its own approval.

## 2026-09-14 (Authentication — password recovery made to work at all, then made to work across devices: PRs #96 and #97, merged, deployed, production Reset Password template switched to `token_hash`, and cross-device recovery V1-verified in production)

A production authentication defect was found on 2026-09-14 while performing the final Remy V1 Google OAuth verification, and it took two PRs to close because the first fix exposed a second, pre-existing limitation. Both are recorded here together because the second cannot be understood without the first. **Nothing in Remy, calendar, Google OAuth scope/consent configuration, the database or onboarding changed in either PR.** `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` is unchanged.

**The observation.** Email delivery and token issuance were fine — a fresh reset email and a fresh magic-link email both arrived — but a fresh reset link landed straight on `/forgot-password?error=link` (*"That reset link has expired or was already used"*), and a magic link opened in a fresh Incognito window redirected to `niteowlhq.com` with credentials in the URL **fragment** while the site stayed visibly signed out.

### PR #96 — `fix(auth): consume Supabase hash auth sessions` (feature commit `406f5fc`, normal merge commit `eeed1f780bb5f24b5efc671e8a90fe9575acf8c5`, 2026-09-14T11:51:36Z; deployed as `niteowl-9hwovkyu6` ● Ready on `niteowlhq.com` / `www`, **SHA-verified** — `githubCommitSha` and the build log's `Cloning … (Commit: eeed1f7)` both read the merge commit)

- **Root cause, with file/line evidence.** Production Supabase answered both email flows with an **implicit-flow redirect** — tokens in the URL fragment (`#access_token=…&refresh_token=…&type=…`) — and NiteOwl had no code path anywhere that could consume that shape. Both auth landing routes were **server** handlers reading only `?code=` (`src/app/auth/callback/route.ts:7`, `src/app/auth/confirm-reset/route.ts:12`); a fragment is never sent to the server, so `confirm-reset` fell through to its "expired" redirect (`route.ts:43`) and the browser carried the still-unconsumed fragment onto the error page. Nothing client-side could take it either: `@supabase/ssr` **hard-codes `flowType: "pkce"`** in `createBrowserClient` (`createBrowserClient.js:37`, not overridable) and auth-js **throws `Not a valid PKCE flow url`** when a PKCE client meets an implicit fragment (`GoTrueClient.js:3181-3184`), so even the pages that instantiate a client refused it — and the homepage instantiates none at all. Not a redirect-before-consume problem, not a cookie-clearing problem, not middleware: the session was never created.
- **The fix, seven files, +668 / −11.** `src/lib/auth/hashSession.ts` (new, pure): `parseAuthHash`, `postAuthHashPath`, `consumeAuthHashSession` — strips the fragment via `history.replaceState` **before** any network call, then hands the tokens to the official `supabase.auth.setSession()`, which verifies the access token against Supabase (or refreshes it) before saving through the existing SSR cookie storage; malformed, error and rejected fragments produce no session; concurrent callers on one page load share one in-flight consumption. `src/app/auth/AuthHashSessionHandler.tsx` (new): render-nothing client component mounted once in the root layout, because Supabase's landing URL is whichever the link named; it creates no client on pages without a fragment and redirects a consumed session to a **fixed literal** (`/reset-password` for `type=recovery`, else `/dashboard`). `reset-password/page.tsx` awaits consumption before its `getUser()` gate so the check cannot race it. `confirm-reset/route.ts`: a landing with **no code** forwards to `/reset-password` (browsers preserve the fragment across a redirect); a failed `?code=` exchange still reports the link expired. `tests/stubs/next-server.mjs` gains an additive `NextResponse.redirect`. The `?code=` path, `/auth/callback`, email/password sign-in, middleware, cookie semantics and every `redirectTo` were unchanged; no localStorage, no parallel auth.
- **Verification.** 29 new tests in `tests/authHashSession.test.mjs` (both flows, fragment stripped before `setSession`, fail closed on error/malformed/rejected/throwing, single in-flight consumption, no token ever reaches the console, route no-code and failed-code paths driven for real); mutation-checked (restoring the old no-code fallback fails exactly one test, with the edit verified to have landed — the CRLF trap); full suite 2074 / 0; `tsc` clean; ESLint 0 problems in changed files; build successful. **Manual preview verification passed for password recovery and normal password sign-in** (a fresh recovery link reached the preview after its domain was added to the Supabase Redirect URL allow-list; new password chosen; new password signed in; authenticated dashboard visible in Incognito). Production after merge: `/api/health` 200 `database: ok`, homepage 200, `confirm-reset` no-code → 307 `/reset-password`, bad code → 307 `/forgot-password?error=link`, `hashSession` markers located in the live client bundle.
- **Then the first real PRODUCTION recovery test FAILED — and PR #96 was working exactly as designed.** Same message, *"expired or already used"*. Read-only diagnosis: that message is now emitted from **one place only**, inside `if (code)` after `exchangeCodeForSession` errored, so the link was a PKCE `?code=` link and the exchange itself was refused. Vercel logs showed exactly one hit on `/auth/confirm-reset`, ~2m45s after the request page loaded — no link-scanner pre-visit, link genuinely fresh, inside the flow-state window, no rate limit. The cause is PKCE by design: auth-js's `_exchangeCodeForSession` (`GoTrueClient.js:1543-1546`) needs the `sb-<ref>-auth-token-code-verifier` cookie, which is **host-only and set only in the browser profile that called `resetPasswordForEmail`**; the link had been opened in a fresh Incognito session. A failed exchange also deletes that cookie (`:1575`), so no retry could succeed either. **Test-procedure at the surface, a genuine product limitation underneath**: a customer who requests a reset on their phone and opens the email on a laptop hits the same wall, so **same-browser-only recovery was ruled not acceptable for V1.**

### PR #97 — `fix(auth): verify token_hash recovery links for cross-device password reset` (feature commit `515fabf`, normal merge commit `8ccddc62d5ce98ebf40d5a260005102f0adcd537`, 2026-09-14T12:21:38Z; deployed ● Ready on `niteowlhq.com` / `www`, **SHA-verified** — build log `Cloning … (Commit: 8ccddc6)`)

- **Supabase's documented server-side pattern, added alongside the existing branch. Three files, +411 / −4.** `src/lib/auth/recoveryLink.ts` (new, pure): `parseRecoveryLinkParams` accepts exactly three link shapes — `token_hash` + `type=recovery`, `code` alone, or nothing — and refuses everything else (hash without type, type without hash, any other type including `RECOVERY` and empty, empty / whitespace / over-512-character hash, `code` + `token_hash` together, `code` + `type`); no network, clock or logging. `src/app/auth/confirm-reset/route.ts`: a `token_hash` link is verified with **`verifyOtp({ type: 'recovery', token_hash })`** on the *same* `createServerClient` + `cookies()` plumbing the `?code=` branch uses, so the session lands in the same SSR cookies; **`type` is a literal in code** and the URL's `type` only gates entry, so a magic-link or signup token can never be relabelled into a recovery session; success → 307 `/reset-password`; expired, reused, malformed or Supabase unreachable → 307 `/forgot-password?error=link`; an invalid combination is refused **before any Supabase call**. The `?code=` exchange (including `code=""` falling to the no-parameter path, matching the original truthiness check), PR #96's no-parameter forwarding, the reset-password page and `hashSession.ts` are unchanged. No token reaches any log, `Location`, error body or console.
- **Verification.** 24 new tests in `tests/authRecoveryTokenHash.test.mjs` (parser matrix; `verifyOtp` success with exactly one POST to `/auth/v1/verify` carrying `type: 'recovery'`; `otp_expired` 403; fetch throwing; wrong/missing type and hash+code refused with **zero** network calls; code-only never hits `/verify`; no-parameter path pinned; no-leak assertion on every route path). Mutation-checked with edits verified to have landed: removing the parser's type gate fails 2, removing the route's invalid-link refusal fails 3; a third mutation (sending the URL's `type` instead of the literal) survives and is **recorded rather than hidden** — the parser has already forced `type === 'recovery'` on that path, so the two are equivalent and the gate is what the first two mutations prove. Full suite **2098 pass / 0 fail / 0 skipped**; `tsc` clean; ESLint 0 problems in changed files (repo baseline unchanged at 11); build successful; `git diff --check` clean. Feature branches for both PRs deleted locally and on origin after production verification.
- **Production route proven live WITHOUT a real token, before any template change:** `GET /auth/confirm-reset?token_hash=synthetic&type=recovery` → 307 `/forgot-password?error=link`, 0-byte body, the synthetic value absent from every response header, the body, and every production log line (logs record only `GET /auth/confirm-reset`); wrong type → `?error=link`; `?code=synthetic` → `?error=link` (unchanged branch); no params → `/reset-password` (PR #96 branch preserved). `/api/health` 200 `database: ok`, homepage 200.
- **Production Supabase configuration change (the only one, performed by the owner AFTER the route was live — the order that matters, because the old route would have bounced every `token_hash` link):** the production project's **Reset Password** email template link was changed from `{{ .ConfirmationURL }}` to **`{{ .SiteURL }}/auth/confirm-reset?token_hash={{ .TokenHash }}&type=recovery`**. A fixed, allow-listed NiteOwl route on the Site URL; the link no longer passes through Supabase's `/verify` redirect at all. Links already in inboxes kept working through the preserved `?code=` branch meanwhile.
- **Cross-device password recovery is V1-VERIFIED in production (2026-09-14).** A fresh reset email was requested; its link was copied into a fresh Incognito session in **another browser context**; it displayed *Choose a new password*; the password update succeeded; after closing that session, a **new** Incognito session signed in with the new password and reached the authenticated dashboard. That is manual-matrix items A/B (request in one context, open in another), D (new password signs in) and E (normal password sign-in unchanged) all passing.

**Deferred, recorded rather than glossed over:**

- **Magic-link authentication is deliberately deferred to V1.1/later and is NOT a V1 blocker.** The app has no magic-link sender (`signInWithOtp` appears nowhere in `src/`), so no customer path issues one; the dashboard's *Send magic link* action necessarily targets the production Site URL, so it could not be exercised on the preview; and PR #96's consumer fails closed (a bad fragment leaves the visitor signed out, never half-authenticated). When it is eventually checked, the procedure is the dashboard action on the test user in a fresh Incognito window, expecting the fragment gone from the address bar and a redirect to `/dashboard`.
- **Known, unchanged exposure:** a `token_hash` link is consumed by any GET, so a corporate mail-scanner pre-visit would burn it. The Supabase-hosted `/verify` link had the same single-use exposure, so this is not new; the mitigation (a click-to-confirm POST page) is V1.1.
- **The temporary preview entry in the Supabase Redirect URL allow-list** (added for the PR #96 preview recovery test) is no longer needed now that the template uses `{{ .SiteURL }}`, and its removal was recommended. **Its removal has not been confirmed as of this record** — confirm and tidy when next in the dashboard; it is harmless meanwhile.
- The unused PKCE code-verifier cookie that `resetPasswordForEmail` still sets is harmless and expires on its own; `ForgotPasswordForm`'s `redirectTo` is now unused by the template and deliberately left as is, since it keeps old-style links working.

## 2026-09-13 (Business Opportunity Scan — opportunity clusters, PR E delivered as PR #94, merged and production verified)

**PR #94** (`feat/scan-opportunity-clusters`, approved head `5df75eba5070ad401ef9cdd17817f3063ca6cef3`, from `main` at `688a42b`; **normal merge commit `4cfd87d1c682914d7a997cdb78e222b997397f1a`** into `main`, 2026-09-13T20:09:21Z). Deployed to production as `dpl_2ZdxdkENNy42SLrXWy7NaecSNGur` (● Ready, target production, built in 42s, carrying `niteowlhq.com` / `www.niteowlhq.com` / `git-main`) and verified. The feature branch was deleted locally and on origin **after** production verification succeeded, with `git merge-base --is-ancestor` proving containment first. **This is PR E, the Part XIV §102 Cluster contract, and it is the smallest of the Scan increments by design: tracing canon rather than filling a table left exactly two relations emittable.** Eight files, exactly as reviewed: two added, six modified, **+1243 / −4**. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` is unchanged — §102 was the contract, and this is the build against it.

- **A cluster is a stated relation between exactly two findings** (§102). It references them by opaque id and **holds no copy**; it is not a finding, not a score, not a container and not a new record type. Two new files: `src/lib/freetools/scanClusters.ts` and `tests/freeToolsScanClusters.test.mjs` (37 tests). Six extended additively: `scanTypes.ts` (the cluster types, `SCAN_CLUSTER_RULE_SET_VERSION`, two `ScanReport` fields), `scanFindings.ts` (**+7 / −0** — one import and two lines on the returned object), `scanPresentation.ts`, `ScanClient.tsx`, `tests/freeToolsScanBoundary.test.mjs` (module list 11 → 12) and `tests/freeToolsScanSurface.test.mjs`.
- **Strictly downstream, and structurally so.** `deriveFindings`, `computeLostRevenue`, `prioritise`, `deriveDependencies`, `deriveEvidenceGaps` and `recommendFor` neither import the cluster module nor take a clusters argument, and a test proves no upstream module imports it — only the assembler does. §102's *"clustering never collapses, summarises, re-ranks or re-confidences a member"* is therefore a property of the dependency graph rather than a promise in prose. **`report.findings` is unchanged in count, content and `SCAN_CONDITION_ORDER`.**
- **Two of five relations are emittable, and the other three are blocked by missing CANON rather than missing code.** `sequential_in_one_process` fires on literally adjacent funnel stages; `independent` is the fallthrough and, per §102, a real output. `shared_cause_candidate` needs intersecting hypothesis sets (§103, which does not exist); `competing_for_same_resource` needs a canonical resource model, and *"every action needs the owner's attention"* is a truism that would fire on every pair; `masked_measurement` needs an upstream integrity gap that makes a downstream **finding's** answers unreliable, and no canon-traceable trigger exists in Phase 1 — Q6 states a practice rather than a measurement, and Q7 concerns conversations that did happen. **No hypothesis, resource or masking model was invented to make any of them fire**, and no proxy stands in: a shared question, a shared cap or a member confidence is not a shared candidate cause. An exhaustive sweep pins that only the two reachable relations and their two rules ever appear, and a source-level test proves the module cannot read a finding's evidence, confidence or cap.
- **Adjacency is LITERAL, and this is the one place restraint cost an output.** The never-assessed `work_booked` stage sits at position 4, so `booking.friction` (3) and `enquiry.no_followup` (5) fall through to `independent` rather than being related across a stage the Scan deliberately refuses to judge. The complete Phase 1 output space: no findings → none; one finding → none (§102: *"a single-finding report has no clusters at all"*); unanswered + friction → one `sequential_in_one_process`; every other pair → `independent`; all three → one sequential and two independent.
- **`independent` means our rules established nothing — never that the findings are unrelated.** Its confidence is deliberately `low`, because *"no relation rule fired"* is a statement about a small and young rule set whose other positive rules cannot fire at all, and a `high` there would turn an absence of evidence into a positive claim. The wording says it out loud: *"That is not the same as knowing they are unrelated — it means no link has been established, and we would rather say so than invent one."*
- **A cluster carries its OWN provenance and its OWN relation confidence and inherits neither** (§102, §92). `source_type` is `derived_deterministic` throughout; the type cannot express `business_provided`, because the owner never told us two findings were related and giving a cluster their evidence would launder our reading into their testimony. Proven not to inherit: `independent` stays `low` where **both** members are `high`, and capping a member to `medium` leaves the sequential relation at `high`. `relation_confidence` is named for what it measures.
- **The relation rules carry their own version** (§107.3, which names *"the relation rules"* alongside the ordering rules). `SCAN_CLUSTER_RULE_SET_VERSION` is a separate constant feeding a separate report field, present on every report including one with no clusters, and neither `SCAN_RULE_SET_VERSION` nor `SCAN_PRIORITISATION_RULE_SET_VERSION` moved.
- **Deterministic throughout.** Repeat runs deeply equal; reversing the caller's finding order yields identical output; members ordered earlier-stage-first; the array ordered by pair stage positions, total, no ties; `cluster_id` derived from the relation plus canonical members, stable, unique and **never an index**. No clock, randomness, identifier generation, network, storage, tenant, provider or model is reachable — the module imports only `scanFunnel` and `scanTypes`, and joins the boundary suite as its twelfth module, inheriting all seventeen structural prohibitions.
- **No new report section.** The established relation renders as one line inside the existing *"How these relate"* area, above the dependency rows; every pair the rules did not relate is covered by **one honest sentence** rather than a row each, which would be padding in the other direction. Reports with fewer than two findings render no cluster content. Finding cards and their order are unchanged; the footer gains only the relation rule-set version.
- **Upstream non-interference was proven, not assumed.** Across twelve scenarios, every PR D field — findings, impact and lost-revenue sizing, recommendations with their next steps, product attribution and handoffs, `impact_class`, funnel, `earliest_leak`, dependencies, prioritisation and evidence gaps — was recomputed directly from the shipped modules with no clusters in play and **deep-compared** against the report. All deep-equal. A suppressed finding (Q4 > Q3) is never clustered.
- **One reviewed scope extension, accepted deliberately.** `tests/freeToolsScanSurface.test.mjs` was not in the permitted-modify list, but its pre-existing **exact-match** footer assertion could not survive the required `cluster_rule_set_version` — confirmed by execution that the old substring is absent once the footer renders four versions. The assertion was **extended, never loosened**: same mechanism, same literal prefix, same order and separators, each version read from its own report field, one requirement added. An alternative existed — rendering the new version as a separate trailing sentence would have preserved the old substring — and was rejected because it orphans one version outside the list purely to preserve a test's literal shape. **Recorded rather than glossed over.**
- **A pre-existing assertion caught a draft wording and the wording changed, not the test.** `/we (observed|saw|measured|found|noticed)/i` matched *"We found no established link"*; the sentence became *"No link has been established"*. The rule exists because §84.2 forbids language implying NiteOwl looked at the business's systems, and it was right to fire.
- **Verification:** cluster suite **37 pass / 0 fail**; PR D suites **124 pass / 0 fail, unmodified**; free-tools **502 pass / 0 fail**; full repository **2045 pass / 0 fail / 0 skipped** across 351 suites; `tsc --noEmit` clean; ESLint **0 problems in PR E files** with the repository baseline unchanged at **11 (7 errors, 4 warnings)**; `next build` successful with the Scan route still **`○ (Static)`**; `git diff --check` clean. Staging was by explicit filename for all eight files — never `git add -A` — and both the staged index and the commit were confirmed to exclude `supabase/.temp/cli-latest`.
- **Production verification after merge:** the deployment began **three seconds** after the merge and reached Ready; `/api/health` HTTP 200 `{"status":"ok","database":"ok"}`; homepage HTTP 200; `/free-tools/business-opportunity-scan` HTTP 200. **Stronger than a status code, all twelve client chunks the Scan page loads were fetched and inspected, and four distinct cluster markers were located in `/_next/static/chunks/3yivmzi59j782.js`** — *"two points on one path"*, *"not the same as knowing"*, *"No link has been established"* and *"relation rules"* — so the sequential wording, the independence denial, the summary sentence and the footer label are all genuinely live rather than merely built. **Deployment-to-merge correspondence is NOT SHA-verified** (`vercel inspect` exposed no Git-source metadata, as for PRs #54 through #92), so identification rests on that three-second adjacency, the production aliases, and the bundle-content proof — the strongest of the three.
- **Nothing else changed.** No persistence, database, schema, migration or RLS; no tenant, account, API route or server action; no model, prompt or provider; no network call, clock or randomness; no analytics. The nine questions, validation, sizing, recommendations, funnel, prioritisation, dependencies, impact classification and evidence gaps are **zero-diff**. **Remy and every unrelated system were untouched**, as were all package, config and schema files.

## 2026-09-13 (Business Opportunity Scan — Enquiry Funnel Diagnosis, PR D delivered as PR #92, merged and production verified)

**PR #92** (`feat/scan-enquiry-funnel-diagnosis`, approved head `3f430567548423b6d52e75fbfa207dadbff7e0a4`, from `main` at `4e92176`; **normal merge commit `97dda2b3c0b07bde0cbe6d1aa30fc9ac35b3fed1`** into `main`, 2026-09-13T16:57:07Z). Deployed to production as `dpl_6cAqdZkmi4sqo6GUe2rEnMbswKv4` (● Ready, target production, carrying `niteowlhq.com` / `www.niteowlhq.com` / `git-main`) and verified. The feature branch was deleted locally and on origin after `git branch -d` accepted it, proving containment. **This is PR D, the Business Opportunity Scan Intelligence Enhancement, implementing the contracts Part XIV defined — and it is the point at which the Scan stops being a questionnaire with findings attached and starts reasoning about the process the owner described.** Seventeen files, exactly as reviewed: eleven added, six modified, **+4312 / −12**. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` is unchanged — Part XIV was the contract, and this is the build against it.

- **Five new pure modules under `src/lib/freetools/`**, each held to the same structural boundary suite as the original six: `scanFunnel.ts`, `scanDependencies.ts`, `scanPrioritisation.ts`, `scanImpactClass.ts` and `scanEvidenceGaps.ts`. `tests/freeToolsScanBoundary.test.mjs` now covers **eleven modules instead of six**, so every one of its seventeen prohibitions — no model, no clock, no randomness, no network, no storage, no tenant, no provider, nothing claiming to be observed — applies to the diagnosis layer too. That widening is the point: the diagnosis layer is where the temptation to reach for a model is greatest.
- **The funnel (§101).** One `business_process`, `enquiry_to_booked_work`, in five stages with opaque ids and fixed positions: `enquiry_received` (1) → `enquiry_answered` (2) → `time_agreed` (3) → `work_booked` (4) → `enquiry_followed_up` (5, marked `recovery`, because follow-up is what happens to an enquiry that did **not** book and therefore sits after the booking decision on the path the owner described). Each stage carries the state of what we know — `owner_declared`, `unknown` with `owner_not_sure` or `not_answered` recorded separately, or `inconsistent` — **separately from its own health**: `finding`, `appears_adequate`, `not_established` with its reason, or `not_assessed`. `derived` is declared and never emitted, and no stage can be `observed`: the type cannot express it.
- **Two stages are deliberately never given a verdict, and this is the restraint the module turns on.** `enquiry_received` reads Q1–Q3, none of which establishes anything alone (§87.2), and how many enquiries a business gets is demand generation — another product's domain. `work_booked` reads Q9, an E1 operand that raises no condition, so asserting a conversion problem there would reach a class §81.3 excluded, and asserting conversion is fine would be the same error with the sign flipped. Both render a plain context note instead. **Adequacy is a claim, so it is refused wherever the answers do not support it** — including Q4 = 0 with Q5 = *no*, where "nothing is missed" rests on visibility the owner has just told us they lack. That is recorded as `not_established` / `no_way_of_knowing`, **not** as an inconsistency: the two statements can both be true.
- **Earliest-leak detection (§100.3).** The earliest **assessable** stage carrying a finding, reported with the stages before it that look adequate **and** the stages before it that were never established. The second list is the honesty clause and is not optional: without it, "earliest" silently implies nothing earlier is wrong, which the answers do not support when an earlier stage was never established at all. The wording says, in as many words, that this is about the order of the path the owner described and **not a claim about what causes what**.
- **Deterministic prioritisation (§100.2), versioned separately.** A four-rung visible ladder — blocking dependency, then position on the enquiry path, then finding confidence, then `SCAN_CONDITION_ORDER` as the canonical final tie-break — where the **first rule that distinguishes two findings places them and supplies the enumerated reason code**, shown to the owner as a fixed sentence. In Phase 1 only *earlier in the enquiry path* and *only finding* ever fire, because the three conditions anchor to three different stages and stage position is already a total order; a sweep over the answer space **pins that the other three never bind**, which is what stops a fourth condition or a second condition on one stage being added silently. `SCAN_PRIORITISATION_RULE_SET_VERSION` is a **separate constant feeding a separate report field**, so an ordering change is visible as one rather than hidden inside a findings change (P47).
- **`report.findings` ordering is UNCHANGED, and prioritisation is a separate list.** The delivery order stays `SCAN_CONDITION_ORDER` and the finding cards still render in it; the order the owner is advised to **act** in is `report.prioritisation`, which references findings by condition code and reorders nothing. The two genuinely differ — with follow-up and friction both present, priority is friction then follow-up, the reverse of the enumeration — which is precisely why the ordering needed its own version.
- **Estimated money cannot reach the ordering, structurally.** `prioritise` is handed findings and dependencies; `ScanFinding` carries no impact, no estimate and no basis, so there is nothing there to order by value even by accident. Pinned three ways: two runs differing **only** in the sizing operands produce deeply equal prioritisation; a larger estimate does not promote its finding; and a source-level test refuses the vocabulary of scoring (`score`, `weight`, `points`, `composite`, `normalised_value`) and any reference to an impact, estimate, basis or currency inside the module. **No score, no weights, no points, no normalised units, no composite number — neither displayed nor computed in memory.**
- **Dependencies (§104).** One entry per pair of findings, in the (earlier, later) direction, as `should_precede` under the canonical rule `stage_order`, ordered by stage position so the caller's order is irrelevant. Zero or one finding produces none. `blocked_by`, `independent` and `should_follow` are **declared and never emitted** in Phase 1 — nothing among the three conditions makes one action impossible until another is done, and all three lie on one ordered path — as are `measurement_integrity`, `capacity_headroom` and `conversion_before_volume`, which have nothing to fire on. A sweep pins all of it. **A dependency is not evidence of causation**, and every sentence says so: *"This is about the order things can be measured in, not about one causing the other."*
- **Four-state impact classification (§106).** `quantified`, `directional`, `material_unquantifiable` and `insufficient_evidence`, mapped from the eleven reason codes the sizing module already produces, as a Record over the closed enumeration so a reason added without a class is a **compile error**. All four are reachable from real reports. It is **presentation only**: the classifier reads the impact and nothing else, has no access to the operands, and therefore **cannot** upgrade `directional` to `quantified` by widening a bucket or relaxing the order-of-magnitude test. A test asserts the impact on every report is byte-identical to `computeLostRevenue`'s own output, and `scanLostRevenue.ts` neither imports nor knows about the classifier. `material_unquantifiable` reads as a judgement — *"we have not tried, rather than guessing"* — and never as a failure.
- **Evidence gaps (§105).** Fourteen codes derived from three sources **already on the report** — the confidence caps the findings display, the reasons sizing gave for not producing a number, and the funnel's own stage states — deduplicated by code and emitted in a fixed declaration order. The raw answers are deliberately **not** read, which is what makes a gap set reconstructible from the report that carries it; a test rebuilds the expected set independently from those three sources and deep-equals it. `expected_information_gain` comes from a closed four-member enumeration whose every member is a claim about **NiteOwl's own rules**, never about the business, and a test refuses any gap whose wording carries a digit, a currency, or the vocabulary of loss. **`no_permitted_expression` produces no gap at all** — no information an owner could supply would size those findings, and a gap there would promise a number that does not exist.
- **One discrepancy found during implementation and corrected before merge, recorded because the mechanism matters.** `unanswered_count_not_given` and `unanswered_count_unknown` were specified to derive from the sizing reasons `q4_missing` / `q4_not_sure` — but **sizing only runs on a finding, and an absent or "not sure" Q4 raises none** (§87.4), so neither reason can ever occur on a report and both codes were unreachable. Verified directly rather than inferred. They are now derived from the **`enquiry_answered` stage state** (`answer_not_given` → the gap naming Q4 as the closing question; `owner_not_sure` → the gap asking the owner to count rather than re-asking a question they answered truthfully), with the approved blocking, information-gain and effort semantics preserved exactly. The Q6 and Q7 gaps moved onto the same stage-keyed table — identical behaviour, **one mechanism instead of two** — so `deriveEvidenceGaps` now reads findings and the funnel and the answers parameter is gone. **No finding is manufactured, sizing still never runs without one, and `scanLostRevenue.ts` is untouched.**
- **The report the owner reads.** New sections in order: *"Where your enquiries are going"* (the funnel, shown on **every** report including a zero-findings one, where a qualified *"from what you told us, this looks like it is working"* is the highest-trust thing the Scan can say); *"Where to start"* (the earliest leak, the ordered priorities each with its reason sentence, and the order-not-cause line); the existing finding cards **in their existing order**, each gaining *"Priority N of M"* and an impact-class line **beside** the untouched impact block; *"How these relate"* (only with two or more findings); and *"What would sharpen this"* (only where gaps exist). *"What you told us"* is unchanged; the footer gains the ordering rule-set version alongside the other two. The surface holds **no rule of its own** — a test refuses any stage table, comparator, gap table or classifier call in `ScanClient.tsx` and requires it to read `report.funnel`, `report.prioritisation`, `report.dependencies` and `report.evidence_gaps`.
- **Anti-funnel properties are executable tests, not review items (P51).** `tests/freeToolsScanAntiFunnel.test.mjs` sweeps the answer space and pins: a zero-findings report stays fully reachable with **empty** prioritisation, dependencies and earliest leak, and renders no priority, dependency, product or handoff; `recommended_product: null` and `free_tool_handoff: null` stay simultaneously reachable on a complete, useful report; prioritisation length always equals findings length and never ranks something without a finding, including a Q4 > Q3 suppressed one; every next step is doable with no purchase; no report asks for an account, a sign-up or payment; **no urgency vocabulary appears anywhere**; and a sized estimate is shown as a per-week range, never annualised and never as a cost of delay.
- **What is preserved, and was verified rather than assumed.** Existing finding detection is unchanged (`deriveFindings` untouched); `report.findings` remains in `SCAN_CONDITION_ORDER`; `SCAN_RULE_SET_VERSION` stays `"v1"` — PR #92 changes no finding, sizing or recommendation rule, so findings-comparability is not broken; zero-findings behaviour is preserved; **the nine questions are untouched** in wording, options, allowed values, requiredness and order. `scanQuestions.ts`, `scanValidation.ts`, `scanLostRevenue.ts` and `scanRecommendations.ts` have **zero diff**. All twelve deleted lines across the PR were audited individually and every one is an in-place widening — a function signature gaining props, a call site, the footer line, the findings map gaining `impact_class`, and two boundary-test lines. **No pre-existing assertion was weakened to make the implementation pass.**
- **Nothing was added that the Scan did not already have.** No persistence, no database, schema, migration or RLS, no tenant or `org_id`, no account, no API route or server action, no model or prompt, no provider dependency, no network call, no clock, no randomness, no analytics or tracking, and no external runtime service. No `DecisionRecord`, Spine event, Business Memory or Business Graph write; no outcome or impact measurement; no Part XIII provenance runtime; no cross-product learning. **No Remy code, test, flag, schema or configuration was touched**, and no package, config, schema or migration changed.
- **Verification before merge:** PR D suites **124 pass / 0 fail**; free-tools suites **455 pass / 0 fail**; full repository **1998 pass / 0 fail / 0 skipped** across 343 suites; `tsc --noEmit` clean; ESLint at the existing baseline of 11 problems with **0 in PR D files**; `next build` successful; `git diff --check` clean. Staging was done by explicit filename for all seventeen files — never `git add -A` — and both the staged index and the resulting commit were confirmed to exclude `supabase/.temp/cli-latest`.
- **Production verification after merge:** the deployment began **two seconds** after the merge and reached Ready in 43s; `/api/health` HTTP 200 `{"status":"ok","database":"ok"}`; homepage HTTP 200; `/free-tools/business-opportunity-scan` HTTP 200. Stronger than a status code, **the deployed client bundle was confirmed to contain the PR #92 funnel implementation** — the string *"Where your enquiries are going"* was located in `/_next/static/chunks/1o1od15vo02n2.js` — so the diagnosis layer is genuinely live rather than merely built. **Deployment-to-merge correspondence is NOT SHA-verified**: `vercel inspect` exposed no Git-source metadata, the same limitation recorded for PRs #54, #58, #60, #62, #66, #68, #70 and #89, so identification rests on that two-second adjacency plus the production aliases. Recorded as the weaker evidence it is.
- **What PR #92 does NOT do, and what its merge does not approve:** clusters (§102) and hypotheses (§103), both Part XIV contracts and both deliberately out of scope; scenario / counterfactual output (**L35**); `evidence_refs[].role` on the shipped evidence shape (**P49**); persistence, run identity, bearer-token linkage and the consent and promotion flow (Part XII §89); outcome or impact measurement and any learning runtime; Q1/Q2-aware routing; Setup Kit pre-fill; the standalone Lost Revenue entry; a second business process; a fourth condition code; and every later Scan phase. **PR D is complete and closed.** Still open and unchanged by this merge: S1–S5 and N.1–N.4 from the PR #84 reviews, PR #87's two cosmetic follow-ups, W.2, the PR #89 keyboard-navigation verification gap, and every V1.1/later Remy item. The next milestone is still Google's verification review.
- **One adjacent case recorded rather than acted on.** Q4 = 0 with Q5 = *no* leaves the answering stage `not_established` / `no_way_of_knowing` with **no evidence gap** — the owner is told the stage could not be established and why, but gets no entry naming what would change that, because no question of ours and no counting exercise closes it: what is missing is a record. That is current intended behaviour and is pinned by test. If a gap is wanted there later, `miss_visibility_absent` already exists with matching semantics, but it is a behaviour decision and was deliberately not taken here.

## 2026-09-12 (Architecture Part XIV — the Business Opportunity Scan's intelligence contracts, and the two boundaries PR D needed closed first)

**Documentation only, and a narrowing rather than an extension of what NiteOwl may claim.** No code, schema, migration, service, API, route, UI, provider, integration, flag, prompt, test or configuration was created or changed; `src/`, `tests/`, `supabase/` and `docs/sql/` were not touched or opened for edit. **Nothing is implemented and nothing is approved for implementation. NOW: none. PR D is NOT started and is not authorised by this part.** Three files: `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` (new Part XIV §100–§108, plus two narrow in-place amendments to §86.1 and §43.3), `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md` (canonical set extended to Parts I–XIV, plus a status paragraph) and `CHANGELOG.md` (this entry).

The Business Opportunity Scan intelligence review concluded **ARCHITECTURE STRENGTHENING REQUIRED FIRST** — not because the product direction was unclear, but because two of the six increments in the approved next enhancement (PR D, *"Enquiry Funnel Diagnosis"*) would have contradicted canon as written. Implementing first and reinterpreting the canon afterwards is the silent redesign this document exists to prevent.

- **Boundary 1 — §86.1's NOT IN list said more than it meant.** It excluded *"a scoring model, ranking model or learned prioritisation"* as one undifferentiated item, which would also have excluded a **deterministic, explainable, versioned, product-scoped** ordering over rules the architecture already holds — something **§43.3 has always permitted every product** (*"Each product ranks in its own domain terms and stores the ranking with the scoring version that produced it"*). The prohibition is **unchanged in substance** and is restated as what it always meant: **no opaque or composite score, no learned or probabilistic ranking model, no hidden weights.** The amendment is marked in place with its date and reason rather than silently rewritten, and **nothing else in that list moves.**
- **§100.2 states the narrow permission and its five conditions**, each load-bearing: deterministic (no model, randomness, clock, tenant or provider reachable); explainable from a visible rule (every position carries the **enumerated reason code of the rule that placed it**, shown to the owner as a sentence); product-scoped (never a NiteOwl priority unit, never comparable with another product's ordering); versioned independently (`prioritisation_rule_set_version`, **separate** from the findings' rule-set version so an ordering change is visible as one); and **no hidden score** — no weights, points, normalised units or composite number, *"neither displayed nor computed internally: a number that exists only in memory is still a score."* **Estimated impact is deliberately not an ordering input** — in Phase 1 exactly one condition of three is ever sizeable (§88.1), so ordering by value would order by what NiteOwl happens to be able to measure. The existing `SCAN_CONDITION_ORDER` enumeration **remains the final tie-break**, so an ordering that distinguishes nothing degrades to today's behaviour.
- **§43.3 gains the matching statement in place**, with the rule that generalises beyond the Scan: **a product-scoped deterministic ordering is versioned like a score, because it is a ranking rule whatever its shape**, and where the ordering is separable from a product's other rules it carries its **own** version rather than sharing the product's.
- **Boundary 2 — the Atlas boundary had never been written down.** §23 already names *"Atlas presenting a `correlated_with` claim as 'X caused Y'"* as a product bug of the most damaging kind, and §25/§44 give Atlas the cross-domain causal charter — but nothing said what the Scan may therefore do. **§100.3 writes it in both directions.** The Scan **MAY** represent the enquiry-to-booked-work process from the owner's own answers, identify ordering and adjacency within it, name the **earliest** stage where work appears to be lost, express hypotheses, identify dependencies, say what does **not** appear to be the immediate problem, and identify evidence gaps. It **MUST NOT** assert a cause, reason across domains, present correlation as causation, represent a process it did not ask about, or become a general business-intelligence engine. Three enforcing rules: **one process only** in Phase 1 (a second is a §81.3 boundary change, not a new stage); **cross-domain synthesis belongs to Atlas** (§42.3) and a Scan finding may be **one input** to a synthesis but never the synthesis; and **adjacency is arithmetic, not causation** — *"an enquiry nobody answered cannot later be followed up"* is a statement about the order of a path the owner described.
- **Six contracts defined, four of them narrowings of what Parts XI–XIII already held.** §101 the **enquiry funnel** with four stage states (`owner_declared` / `derived` / `unknown` / `inconsistent`) and the rule that **no stage is ever `observed`** — the Scan observes nothing, and a type that cannot express an observation cannot accidentally claim one; a stage with no finding renders as **adequate, not empty**, because *"silence is not the same statement"*. §102 **opportunity clusters** with five relations, each cluster carrying **its own provenance and confidence and inheriting neither** (a relation can be weaker than both things it relates), findings keeping their own evidence inside a cluster, and `independent` as a **real output** because a cluster is never invented to create a narrative. §103 the **hypothesis**, reusing §42.2's existing `hypotheses[]` (*"a ranked list, not a winner"*) and adding no new type, with four claim classes kept distinguishable **in the type and not only in the wording** — `observation` unreachable, `business_state` reachable, `hypothesis` the Scan's ceiling, `asserted_cause` **forbidden to the Scan in every phase**. §104 **dependencies** (`blocked_by` / `should_precede` / `should_follow` / `independent`), each naming the canonical rule that produced it, and **a dependency is not evidence of causation**. §105 **evidence gaps**, derived and never primary, with the rule that `expected_information_gain` is **a claim about NiteOwl's own rules, never about the business** — *"this would let us put a range on it"* is verifiable from the sizing gates; *"this would probably reveal £X"* is an estimate wearing a gap's clothing and Part X **P39** bars it. §106 the **four-state impact classification** (`quantified` / `directional` / `material_unquantifiable` / `insufficient_evidence`) as a **presentation contract over the eleven reason codes the sizing module already produces** — no arithmetic, no gate change, no new number — with `directional` explicitly **never silently upgradable to `quantified`** by widening a bucket or relaxing the order-of-magnitude test.
- **§107 preserves the chain and the stack by reference rather than restating them.** Part XIV deepens stages 2–5 (business state, diagnosis, recommendation, evidence) and **produces nothing at stages 6–10**: no authority granted, no action, no outcome measured, no impact asserted, nothing learned. Every contract carries **its own provenance, confidence and timestamp and inherits none of the three** (§92). One constraint is stated plainly because the Scan's shape invites the opposite assumption: **the Scan cannot close the learning loop by itself** — it holds stated answers, and a second stated answer is not a measurement, so a **measured** outcome must come from a product that observes (§86.1 **L32**, which **P41** must precede).
- **§107.4 restates the anti-funnel rule as properties the contracts must have rather than intentions**, because this part raises funnel risk: a **zero-findings report stays fully reachable** with **empty** prioritisation, clustering, dependency and scenario output; `recommended_product: null` and `free_tool_handoff: null` stay **simultaneously** reachable with a complete report; **prioritisation ranks genuine findings only** and may never manufacture, promote or retain a finding in order to have something to rank; at least one action must be doable with no purchase; no urgency vocabulary without owner-stated urgency; and *"no clear opportunity found"* is **a designed result, not a fallback**.
- **Classification: NOW none.** Five PREPARE items — **P47** an ordering is versioned separately from the rules it orders; **P48** stage, cluster and dependency references are opaque ids, never array positions (*"an index is not a reference"*); **P49** `evidence_refs[].role` reaches the Scan's evidence shape (§93.2 **T3**), unrecoverable after the run; **P50** a hypothesis can never be stored as a cause, its claim class and tier travelling with it; **P51** §107.4's anti-funnel properties are **structural tests, not review items**, because *"a safeguard that is not executable is an intention."* One LATER item — **L35** scenario / counterfactual output, triggered only once the evidence and impact-classification surfaces ship, because *"before that, a scenario is a forecast with better manners."*
- **What this part does NOT do:** it creates no module (`scanFunnel.ts`, `scanPriority.ts`, `scanDependencies.ts`, `scanImpactClass.ts` and `scanEvidenceGaps.ts` do not exist), changes no live Scan behaviour, changes none of the nine questions, does not touch `ScanReport`, `ScanClient.tsx`, `scanPresentation.ts` or `scanValidation.ts`, bumps no runtime rule version, adds no inconsistency code, modifies no test, adds no persistence, model or provider dependency, and touches no schema or runtime configuration. `docs/AGENT_ACCESS_LAYER.md` is unchanged. **PR D remains not started**, and the next milestone is still Google's verification review.
- **One correction made during drafting, recorded because it is the kind of error that survives review:** the first draft of §100.1 cited the Atlas charter to §36 and §30, and the `correlated_with` rule to §46. Verified against the document, the actual sections are **§25** (moat mapping), **§44** (solutions not reports) and **§23** (action → outcome linking without lying about causation). All three references were corrected before commit. A cross-reference to a section that does not say what it is cited for is worse than no reference.

## 2026-09-12 (Business Opportunity Scan Phase 1 — the first user-facing surface, PR #89 merged and production verified)

**PR #89** (`feat/scan-phase1-surface`, approved head `61b82f9`, **normal merge commit `f3ab620acbbfc923d6ee2d4590fcaba80a5e3301`** into `main`, 2026-09-12T21:24:41Z). Deployed to production as `dpl_7m7mdyDGdTk3H4BzedgEUxJNEY9o` (● Ready, target production, carrying `niteowlhq.com` / `www.niteowlhq.com` / `git-main`) and smoke-verified. The feature branch was deleted locally and on origin after `git branch -d` accepted it, proving containment. **This is the first time the Business Opportunity Scan is something a visitor can actually use.** Five files, exactly as reviewed: `src/app/free-tools/business-opportunity-scan/page.tsx`, `ScanClient.tsx` and `scanPresentation.ts` (all new), `tests/freeToolsScanSurface.test.mjs` (new, 705 lines), and `src/app/free-tools/page.tsx` (+11 −1, the listing entry). No `src/lib/freetools/` module changed: **PR #84's and PR #87's pure logic is consumed, not modified.**

- **What it is.** A three-screen client-side questionnaire over the canonical nine questions, rendering the deterministic report the existing modules already produce. Screen 1 Q1–Q2 ("How enquiries reach you"), screen 2 Q3–Q7 ("What happens to them"), screen 3 Q8–Q9 ("Sizing it (optional)") — split by **position** in the contract order, never by question id, so the contract remains the single source of truth. `validateScanAnswers` is the sole authority on refusal; the surface never repairs, coerces or second-guesses an answer.
- **It stores nothing.** No `localStorage`, no `sessionStorage`, no cookie, no query string, no API route, no database write and no network call. The answers live in React state for one page view, and the intro says so before a single question is asked. Refreshing clears the scan — verified manually in production-equivalent preview. This satisfies §26's staged model trivially, exactly as the Setup Kit does.
- **Manual preview verification against the approved head, all passed:** Chrome desktop flow end to end; required-question validation with visible errors; Back / Next behaviour; "Not sure" present and selectable on Q3, Q4 and Q8; Q8 currency has **no default** and must be deliberately chosen, including the "Other" three-letter code path; refresh clears state; a sized report rendering **EUR 140–210 per week** with **both** confidence statements (finding and estimate) reading High and shown separately; the zero-findings report neutral and still carrying "What you told us"; `booking.friction` producing **exactly one** Setup Kit handoff whose URL is the bare `/free-tools/ai-receptionist-setup-kit` with no query or hash, opening the Kit **blank with no Scan data carried over**; all four Q8 refusal cases (missing currency, inverted range, zero amount, and the report then succeeding once corrected); Save a copy opening the browser print dialog; Start again resetting to the beginning; and Samsung / Chrome mobile looking and working normally.
- **KEYBOARD-ONLY NAVIGATION WAS NOT PERFORMED.** It is recorded as an **unperformed manual check, not a pass**, and is a **documented non-blocking accessibility verification gap** carried to V1.1/later. The non-blocking judgement rests on code evidence, never on a substituted result: the surface uses native interactive elements throughout (`<button>` for every action, native radio / checkbox inside `<label>`, native `<select>`, native text inputs, `<Link>` for navigation), has **no `tabIndex` override anywhere**, has **no `onClick` on a non-interactive element**, and contains no custom widget, modal or focus trap; errors already carry `role="alert"`, `aria-invalid` and `aria-describedby`, and questions use `<fieldset>` with `aria-required`. Keyboard operability therefore follows from the element choices rather than from bespoke handling — but it has not been observed, and must not be written up as though it had.
- **One review observation, deliberately no code change.** `finish()` computes `firstBad` and jumps to the first screen holding an invalid answer, but that cross-screen jump is **unreachable through the UI**: every reachable validation error attaches to its own question (the sole cross-question rule, Q4 > Q3, is surfaced as an *inconsistency*, not an error), and `next()` re-gates each screen on the way out, so an error cannot be carried forward. The branch is defensive code behaving correctly, not a defect. The gate itself was verified working in all four Q8 refusal cases, where `firstBad` correctly resolves to the screen the visitor is already on. **No correction was proposed and none was needed.**
- **Production smoke check (minimal, not a repeat of the manual suite):** homepage HTTP 200; `/api/health` HTTP 200 `{"status":"ok","database":"ok"}`; `/free-tools` HTTP 200 and linking to the Scan; `/free-tools/business-opportunity-scan` HTTP 200 and serving the intro copy. **Deployment-to-merge correspondence is NOT SHA-verified** — `vercel inspect` exposed no Git-source metadata, the same limitation recorded for PRs #54, #58, #60, #62, #66, #68 and #70 — so identification rests on the production deployment appearing **two seconds** after the merge (21:24:43Z against 21:24:41Z) and carrying the production aliases. Recorded as the weaker evidence it is.
- **What PR #89 did NOT do, and what its merge does not approve:** persistence of a run; run identity or bearer-token linkage; the consent and promotion flow (Part XII §89); any database, schema, migration or RLS; tenant or `org_id` identity; a `DecisionRecord`, Spine event, Business Memory or Business Graph write; outcome or impact measurement; any Part XIII provenance runtime; cross-product learning; any model call, prompt or provider change; Setup Kit pre-fill; Q1/Q2-aware routing; the standalone Lost Revenue entry; and every later Scan phase. No Remy code, test, flag, schema or configuration was touched, and `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` is unchanged.
- **Still open and unchanged by this merge:** S1–S5 and N.1–N.4 from the PR #84 reviews, PR #87's two cosmetic follow-ups, W.2, Q1/Q2-aware routing, and every V1.1/later Remy item. **PR D has NOT started**, and the Business Opportunity Scan Intelligence Enhancement has not been specified or begun.

## 2026-09-11 (Business Opportunity Scan Phase 1 recommendation layer — PR #87 merged, deployed and production-health verified)

**PR #87** (`feat/scan-phase1-recommendations`, approved head `21e0ea3`, from `main` at `61d8d2c`; **normal merge commit `6055f6448cdc7ff4f22a728ff382c8f6b3997fc4`** into `main`, 2026-09-11T14:45:03Z). Deployed to production as `dpl_GWa2YAHEagFrBzYM2Zwv4DJbAmwE` (READY, target production, `githubCommitSha` = the merge commit itself, carrying `niteowlhq.com` / `www` / `git-main`) and health-verified: `/api/health` HTTP 200 `{"status":"ok","database":"ok"}`, homepage HTTP 200. The feature branch was deleted locally and on origin after `git merge-base --is-ancestor` proved containment. **PR #87 shipped ONLY the Phase 1 deterministic recommendation layer — pure logic; THE SCAN PRODUCT ITSELF IS NOT YET BUILT.** Two files added: `src/lib/freetools/scanRecommendations.ts` and `tests/freeToolsScanRecommendations.test.mjs` (44 tests). Three extended additively: `scanTypes.ts` (the `ScanRecommendation`, `ScanSuccessCriterion`, `ScanStatedBaseline`, `ScanFreeToolHandoff` and `ScanRecommendedProduct` types, and a `recommendation` field on `ScanReportFinding`), `scanFindings.ts` (`buildScanReport` attaches `recommendFor(finding, answers)` to each finding after sizing — one import, one line) and `tests/freeToolsScanBoundary.test.mjs` (the sixth module joins the structural suite). No other file changed; nothing outside the six modules imports them.

- **What it is.** Every rendered finding carries EXACTLY ONE recommendation, keyed by `finding.condition` and by nothing else — not impact, estimate size, either confidence, cap reason, provider, tenant, clock or model. A sized and an unsized instance of the same condition receive deeply equal recommendations (pinned). No finding → no recommendation; a Q4 > Q3 suppressed finding gets none. All owner-facing text is **fixed data pinned verbatim** by test; every `why_it_matters` begins *"You told us"*, and a forbidden-wording test refuses loss claims, *we observed / we saw*, *most businesses*, benchmarks, currency amounts, percentages and annualised values.
- **Routing (anti-funnel).** `enquiry.unanswered` → `recommended_product: "remy"`; `enquiry.no_followup` → `null`, no handoff; `booking.friction` → `recommended_product: "remy"` — where the answer lives, per §84.3's class table (booking and scheduling friction → Remy) — **and** a **link-only** Setup Kit handoff as the first step of §84.4's path (`{ tool, href: "/free-tools/ai-receptionist-setup-kit", label }` — no query, no answers, no pre-fill, no token, no state, no consent implied; the object on a report is byte-identical to the constant). A test pins that at least one recommendation structurally routes to no paid product.
- **D-B1 — direction-aware improvement from the owner's STATED baseline.** `success_criterion = { metric, direction, threshold_rule: "improvement_from_stated_baseline", baseline, wording }`; there is no target field and no number is ever invented. Metrics: `unanswered_enquiries_per_week` (Q4, **decrease**), `answered_enquiries_becoming_work_share` (Q9 — *of the enquiries you do answer*, so the name and wording carry Q9's denominator; **increase**), `messages_to_book` (Q7, **decrease**). The baseline is the owner's raw answer quoted as `{ question_id, raw_answer, source_type: "business_provided" }` — never `EstimateBasis.result`, never the E1 estimate, never a benchmark or midpoint — and is `null` whenever the stated answer has no position on the metric's scale (`not_sure`, absent, `varies_a_lot`), with fixed wording that says a baseline must be established first. **The criterion is declared, never evaluated, in this layer**: comparing a later answer against the baseline is the `observed outcome → comparison → learning` stage of §85.1's loop and belongs to a later runtime with its own provenance (§43.4, §94). A comparator written during implementation was removed at the final audit; no evaluator replaces it.
- **Canonical metadata.** `action_status: "proposed"`, `authority_level: "recommend"`, `source_type: "derived_deterministic"`, `rule_set_version = SCAN_RULE_SET_VERSION`, `review_window_days: 28`. **No `expected_effect`, no `review_at`, no impact copy** — W.2 stays deferred; the finding's own `impact` sits beside the recommendation on the report and is not duplicated.
- **Verification.** Recommendation suite 44/44; all Scan suites 213/213; full suite **1807/1807** across 301 suites; `tsc --noEmit` clean; ESLint clean on every changed file and the full-repo baseline **unchanged at 11** (7 errors, 4 warnings, all pre-existing and outside the PR); `next build` clean (46/46 pages). Boundary suite: the new module imports only `scanTypes`, reaches no network, storage, environment, clock, randomness, tenant or provider, and exposes no seam for a model. Three mutations applied and caught before the audit (direction-blind threshold → 3 failures; substituted zero baseline on `not_sure` → 5; `enquiry.no_followup` routed to Remy → 1); the module was restored byte-for-byte. **PR #84's 169 tests are unchanged and green**, and PR #85/#86 documentation was not touched.
- **What it is NOT, and what remains not built or approved:** a route, page or UI; persistence; a database, schema, migration or RLS; tenant or org identity; bearer-token linkage; a TTL namespace; the consent and promotion flow; a `DecisionRecord`, Spine event, Business Memory or Business Graph write; outcome or impact measurement; any Part XIII provenance runtime; cross-product learning; Decision Intelligence; model calls or prompts; benchmarks; scoring beyond the existing enumeration; Setup Kit pre-fill; later Scan phases; the standalone Lost Revenue entry; any Remy or provider change. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` is unchanged; S1–S5, N.1–N.4 and W.2 remain open and unresolved.
- **Final read-only audit before commit, and the four corrections it required (all landed in `21e0ea3` before the merge):** (1) `booking.friction` had named no product; canon (§84.3 and the `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md` routing table, §81.2, §84.4) names Remy as where the answer lives, with the Setup Kit as the handoff and the next step useful without either — corrected to `"remy"`. (2) the Q9 metric was named `enquiries_becoming_work_share`, dropping Q9's *answered* denominator — renamed and reworded. (3) a `compareWithStatedBaseline` evaluator pre-empted the comparison stage — removed with its nine tests. (4) status wording that would have contradicted itself once committed — replaced. `enquiry.no_followup`'s Q9 baseline stands: Q9 is the only owner-stated increase-type quantity among the nine questions and is optional, so this baseline is the one most often `null`; the Q4 `not_sure` guard stands as total-function behaviour (unreachable via `buildScanReport` only because §87.4 suppresses the finding first) and keeps its direct test.
- **Two non-blocking cosmetic follow-ups from the pre-merge review, deliberately left in the merged code and to be tidied separately, never as a side effect of other work:** a comment above the ordinal scales in `scanRecommendations.ts` still says answers can "be compared" and that "the comparison is by order alone" (comment only; the module compares nothing), and `enquiry.no_followup`'s `next_step` ends "how many of your enquiries become work" where the criterion wording carries Q9's exact denominator, "the enquiries you answer" (action prose, not the measure; the pinned test would move with it).
- **Deferred, recorded so it is not lost:** §87.2 says Q1/Q2 *"shape the recommendation and the routing"* (so phone answering is not recommended to a form-only trade). PR #87's approved scope is condition-keyed routing per §84.3's class table; **Q1/Q2-aware routing is NOT implemented and remains future work**, to be decided deliberately rather than added as a side effect. Also still open after this merge, unchanged: S1–S5, N.1–N.4, W.2 (estimate vs Impact), the `requested_service` identity, basis terminology and outcome cardinality/history.

## 2026-09-11 (Business Opportunity Scan Phase 1 pure logic — PR #84 shipped, deployed and closed out)

**PR #84** (`feat/scan-phase1-pure-logic`, head `6fa00a7`, **normal merge commit `7d242d8`** into `main`), deployed to production (`niteowl-jt2dqcz0i`, READY, `githubCommitSha` = the merge commit) and health-verified (`/api/health` HTTP 200 `{"status":"ok","database":"ok"}`, homepage HTTP 200). Nine files, all new: `src/lib/freetools/scanTypes.ts`, `scanQuestions.ts`, `scanValidation.ts`, `scanFindings.ts`, `scanLostRevenue.ts` and `tests/freeToolsScanBoundary.test.mjs`, `freeToolsScanFindings.test.mjs`, `freeToolsScanLostRevenue.test.mjs`, `freeToolsScanValidation.test.mjs` (169 tests). Nothing outside those files changed; nothing imports them yet.

- **What shipped — the Phase 1 contract logic of `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` Parts XI–XII, and only that.** The canonical nine questions, allowed values and six-required / three-optional split (§87.2); a validator that refuses and never repairs or coerces, with `not_sure` first-class and Q4 > Q3 surfaced as an inconsistency rather than resolved (§87.4); the deterministic finding engine over the three condition codes, ranked by the enumeration alone (§87.1, §87.3); the Phase 1 Lost Revenue sizing — E1 `lost_revenue.unanswered_enquiries.v1` only, a range never a point, per week, the three assumptions shown, UNKNOWN as a first-class result with its own reason code for the other two conditions and for every §88.3 gate; and the frozen `estimate_basis` with an executable `recomputeFromBasis` / `basisReproducesResult`, so a displayed number is reproducible from its basis alone (§88.4).

## 2026-09-11 (Architecture Part XIII — Decision, Outcome and Impact Provenance, and the one stage the chain was missing)

**Documentation only, and a strengthening rather than a redesign.** No code, schema, migration, service, API, route, UI, provider, integration, flag, prompt, test or configuration was created or changed; `src/`, `tests/`, `supabase/` and `docs/sql/` were not touched or opened for edit. **Nothing is implemented and nothing is approved for implementation. NOW: none.** Three files: `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` (new §91–§99, plus in-place extensions to §20.7's field table and rules and a one-line pointer in §23), `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md` (canonical set extended to Parts I–XIII, plus a status paragraph) and `CHANGELOG.md` (this entry).

The brief asked for Decision Provenance, Outcome Provenance and Impact Provenance as explicit, provider-neutral, cross-product contracts, and for the canonical chain that joins them. **Twelve of the fifteen required outputs already existed** in Parts III–XII as fields, rules and worked examples — `DecisionRecord` (§20.7), the nine source types (§20.6), the five link tiers (§23), M15's as-of evidence, `action_status` (§56.1), `evidence_scope` (§57.1), §59.2's *a chain is a reconstruction, not an entity* — and are consolidated and cross-referenced rather than rewritten. Three were real, labelled **T1–T3**:

- **T1 — the architecture had an outcome and no impact (§95).** `appointment.booked` and *"£640 of revenue"* are two assertions with two evidential standards, and the second had nowhere to live except an estimate P39 forbids from ever becoming a measured result — which meant a number written next to an event could become indistinguishable from the event. The **Impact assertion** is defined as the attribution row §23 always required, given its full shape: `impact_category` (an open, versioned vocabulary, never the last word), `direction` with `unknown` as a recorded result, `quantity` + `unit` as a range never a fabricated point, `time_window` never annualised, **`measurement_basis`** (`measured` / `estimated` / `projected` / `inferred`), its own `provenance`, `link_tier`, assumptions, confidence, `evidence_scope`, `measured_by_product` and `supersedes`. Five rules: never an event or an outcome; only `measured` + `observed`/`derived_deterministic` is learnable; **opposing impacts are both kept and never netted**; no cross-product currency (§43.3's refusal applied to money); every surface shows basis, tier and window. §20.7 gains **rule 9**: *an outcome is not an impact, and the record holds only the first.*
- **T2 — the Outcome group could not say *partial*, *contested*, *reversed* or *not yet due* (§94).** Five of the brief's eight outcome states mapped to existing vocabulary; three did not. `outcome_resolution` (`pending` — the write-time default, so *not yet* is distinguishable from *nobody looked* — / `measured` / `partial` / `unresolved` / `contested` / `reversed`) and `outcome_contradicting_evidence` are added. Learnability is unchanged and still derived, never a flag: rule 5's provenance test **and** `measured`. `reversed` never deletes, edits or re-grades the original — a reversal is a new row referencing it.
- **T3 — three questions the Decision Provenance contract could not answer (§93.2).** `evidence_refs[].role` (`relied_on` / `considered` / `contradicting`, defaulting to the weaker claim) separates what was *available* from what the decision *turned on*; `approval_status` adds `expired` (not `denied`, never scored as a human saying no) and `overridden` (the human's alternative action is a new decision with its own outcome slot, which is what makes *"the owner did X instead and X worked"* learnable); `supersedes` says what replaced a superseded record.

**The chain is stated once (§92): ten stages, each mapped to the carrier it already has, no new record type, no chain table.** Products contribute stages, not chains; the bindings are `correlation_id`, canonical entity references, `addresses_finding_id`, `evidence_refs`, `outcome_refs` and the impact row's references. Ten invariants are consolidated in §92.3. The governing principle: **every stage carries its own provenance, confidence and timestamp, and no stage inherits any of the three from the stage before it.** Hidden model chain-of-thought is never stored on any stage — `explanation` is a business-level summary, `reason_codes` are the learnable form, hypotheses and assumptions are structured claims with tiers.

Also mapped, without new mechanism: temporal integrity as six separate instants and §54.2's two cut-offs (§96); Business Graph as context never audit log, with Part X N1 applied to customer systems as measurement sources (§97.1); the Memory → provenance → measured-outcome learning → Decision Intelligence stack, with *memory is not intelligence; orchestration is not intelligence* adopted as binding (§97.2); the ten governed-agent distinctions, with **authority never inferred from a technical integration permitting an action** (§97.3); provider independence extended to the two new stages under S1 — *a provider is an attribute of an outcome or impact, never a dimension* (§97.4); the brief's nine failure and contradiction cases (§97.5); and the ten-step test a future product must pass to join the chain with no redesign (§97.6). **The eight product boundaries — Remy, Ledger, Atlas, Scout, Pulse, Forge, Nova, Beacon — are intact**, and no external provider owns or is the sole representation of any stage.

**No contradictions requiring a design change.** Three ambiguities recorded: §23's attribution row was underspecified, not wrong; `outcome_quality` was doing two jobs and now has a sibling for the second; Part XI/XII's estimate discipline was already an Impact assertion for one product, and §95 generalises it without changing it. One future hardening noted and not started: provenance rows referencing a `service` will need the deferred `requested_service` canonical identity rather than a label.

**Bands:** three PREPARE items (**P44** outcome resolution fields, **P45** the three T3 fields, **P46** the Impact assertion shape and core vocabulary — all triggering with L18 or the first measured impact, not before), one LATER (**L34** owner-facing chain view), X1–X6 unchanged. **NOW: 0.** Remy V1's locked Definition of Done, its runtime behaviour, the phone/voice, calendar, booking, dashboard, lead-capture and callback paths, every provider and every integration are untouched, and the next milestone is still Google's verification review.

## 2026-09-09 (Architecture Part XII — the Scan's three Phase 1 decisions, and the two revenue calculations that cannot honestly be done)

**Documentation only, and decisions rather than a plan.** No code, schema, migration, service, API, route, UI, provider, integration, flag, prompt, test or configuration was created or changed; `src/`, `tests/`, `supabase/` and `docs/sql/` were not touched or opened for edit. **Nothing is implemented and nothing is approved for implementation.
